# Desert Temple
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs
[Desert Temple]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]); [[Heart of the Forest]] Tier 4
Music: "Torrhus Canyon ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Torrhus Canyon]]
Coordinates: -610 43 244
The Desert Temple is a [[Locations|Location]] found on the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. It can be accessed from the bottom of the [[Torrhus Springs]], past the [[Spring Depths]], and after completing a puzzle.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Desert Temple, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Desert Temple
■ The Altar of an ancient being.
## Features
### First Puzzle
The first puzzle, outside the temple
The Desert Temple can be entered after completing a puzzle outside of it. It features 12 colored buttons and 5 colors on the bottom. Players must click the buttons in order of which colors appear least often to most often, which is Cyan, Yellow, Red, Brown, and Orange.
### Second Puzzle
The second puzzle, inside the temple
After entering the temple, another puzzle must be completed. It features a statue of a bird, a snake, a frog, and a fly. The statues can be rotated by clicking on them. They must be rotated so that:
- The bird faces the snake,
- The snake faces the frog,
- The frog faces the fly,
- and the fly faces away from the other statues, towards a wall.
### Temple Altar
The altar room, past the second puzzle
Upon rotating the statues properly, a door will open, and the player will gain access to the Temple Altar, and permanently gain the passive effects from its Will. Players can donate to become a Benefactor, making [[Black Holes]] and [[Huntraps]] work 25% faster, and granting 2x [[Hunting Fortune]] from the Will bonus. Upon donating, the player will lose their status as a Benefactor of the [[Forest Temple]] if they were one, however, the time that this donation would have lasted will not be lost and rather added to the time as Desert Temple Benefactor.
|Duration|Donation Cost|
|-|-|
|1 Day|70,000 [[Coins]] 2x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|
|5 Days|350,000 [[Coins]] 10x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|
|10 Days|700,000 [[Coins]] 20x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|
|20 Days|1,400,000 [[Coins]] 40x [[Enchanted Helix Log]]|
## Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Solving the first puzzle and opening the template entrance door|The guardian of the temple opens the door...|
|Entering the Desert Temple|The door slams down behind you...|
|Solving the second puzzle and entering the alter room|The statues click into place as a secret passage reveals itself... ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬ STARBORN TEMPLE The Desert Starborn Temple is activated! You now permanently gain the passive effect from its Will. Visit the Altar to become a Benefactor for bonus effects! ▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬▬|
|Donating to the Desert Starborn|BENEFACTOR: You donated to the Forest Temple and will receive its bonus for +#d!|
## Trivia
- There is a 0.390625% (1 in 256) chance for the second puzzle to already be solved when entering.

# Diamond Reserve
Categories: Locations, Mining, Deep Caverns, Quests, Ores
[Diamond Reserve]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 2
Location: [[Deep Caverns]]
← Previous Location: [[Slimehill]]
Next Location →: [[Obsidian Sanctuary]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Lift Operator]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Miner Zombie]] [[Miner Skeleton]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bone]] [[Bone Dye]] [[Exp Share Core]] Miner Armor Piece ([[Miner Armor]]) [[Rotten Flesh]]
Resources: [[Cobblestone]] [[Diamond]]
[Metadata]
ID: DEEP_CAVERNS_ROOM_5
The Diamond Reserve is the fifth floor of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Diamond Reserve, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Diamond Reserve
■ The Diamond Reserve can now be traveled to via the Lift
■ Mine Diamond Ore.
■ The Miner Zombies and Skeletons are highly dangerous!
## Features
The Diamond Reserve features a few attractions.
### Lift Operator
In front of the lift shaft is the [[Lift Operator]], who allows players to teleport between layers of the [[Deep Caverns]] with ease. Once the player has entered the Diamond Reserve, it becomes available as a destination in the [[Lift Operator]]'s menu.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Diamond Reserve:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Lift Operator]]|[[Lift Operator]]|Diamond Reserve; 45.5, 38, 15.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to teleport to other parts of the [[Deep Caverns]], or the [[Dwarven Mines]].|✕|
## Fairy Souls
There are 2 [[Fairy Souls]] found in the Diamond Reserve:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|DC-17|-21|25|72|Cave to Obsidian Sanc.|✔|
|DC-18|-60|37|52|To the right from entrance|✔|
## Trivia
- Mining near the entrance to the [[Obsidian Sanctuary]] or [[Slimehill]] makes it easy to collect [[Diamond|Diamonds]] with a low chance of being attacked.

Divan's Gateway is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]].
## Purpose
Divan's Gateway serves as a bridge connecting the [[Cliffside Veins]] to the [[Great Ice Wall]]. It overlooks [[The Mist]].
A [[Powder Ghast]] and [[Raffle]] have a chance to spawn in Divan's Gateway.

# Dojo
Categories: Locations, Crimson Isle
[Dojo]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Blockjitsu ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crimson Isle]]
Coordinates: -235 108 -597
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Master Tao]]
The Dojo is a collection of Minigames on the [[Crimson Isle]]. The minigames can be completed by talking to [[Master Tao]] and achieving enough points.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Dojo, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dojo
■ Master the trainings.
■ Compare your skills with other players.
■ Earn your Black Belt.
## Location
The Dojo is in a secluded area on a small hill. To the left of the Dojo is a small pool of lava, where players have a chance to fish up a [[Trophy Fish|Karate Fish]]. To get to the Dojo, there is a path leading to it, with small bells on stands scattered around the trail. [[Master Tao]] is the master of the Dojo. He offers a selection of minigames, called tests. Each test is unique, as they all have different objectives. Next to [[Master Tao]] there is a leaderboard showing the 10 Best Scores for each of the games.
## Dojo Tasks
To begin a challenge, the player must speak to Master Tao. The player starts off with only the Test of Force unlocked. To start doing a challenge, the player must not have any items in their inventory or have armor equipped. Pets are disabled during a challenge.
When a player starts a challenge, they will have a brief moment of blindness, before being teleported to the Dojo Arena. The [[SkyBlock Menu]] will be replaced with a Cancel challenge item. The player can click with this item to leave the challenge at any point in time, however, canceling a challenge will be marked as a fail.
There is a leaderboard tracking the Top 10 best scores of each challenge. The player can toggle the leaderboard to show the top scores for each challenge.
Scoring 1000+ (S) marks on any Dojo Challenge rewards SkyBlock XP, which increases [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock Level]].
### Test of Force
Challenge Rules: 1. Knock mobs into the lava. 2. Some mobs give more points than others. 3. Avoid negative point mobs!
The player spawns on a platform, where zombies spawn. The zombies do not damage the player. The aim of the challenge is for the player to hit as many zombies into the lava, without hitting negative point mobs. Zombies with iron helmets reward the player 10 points, zombies with gold helmets reward the player with 20 points, and baby zombies with diamond helmets reward the player with 30 points. The zombies also display how many points they give above their heads. Hitting a negative point zombie will subtract the points it removes from the player's current score.
### Test of Stamina
Challenge Rules: 1. Jump through the open holes in the moving walls. 2. Earn points by staying on the platform. 3. Don't get pushed into the lava!
The player spawns on a platform where moving walls cross it every few seconds. The challenge gets increasingly difficult, with the color of the walls changing respectively to the current difficulty. There are holes in through with the player must walk through or jump through. Getting pushed into the lava or touching the walls will end the challenge. The player earns points throughout the time they stay on the platform.
### Test of Mastery
Challenge Rules: 1. Shoot the targets before they disappear. 2. Green targets are worth 8 points. 3. Yellow targets are worth 16 points. 4. Red targets are worth 32 points.
The player spawns on a small platform, with a bow in their inventory. Surrounding the platform are wooden pillars, upon which Green wool spawns on. After a few seconds, the Green wool will become Yellow. After a few more seconds, the Yellow wool becomes Red, just for a few seconds before it disappears. The player must shoot these wool blocks to earn points. After 2 minutes, the challenge ends.
### Test of Discipline
Challenge Rules: 1. Hit the mob with the correct sword. 2. Every 30 seconds, gain a new type of sword. 3. Don't hit the mob with the wrong sword.
The player spawns on a large platform upon which zombies spawn. The player is automatically given a Wooden Sword. After some time, they are given an Iron Sword, then a Gold Sword and finally a Diamond Sword. The player must hit the correct zombie with the correct sword type. A zombie with a Leather Helmet must be hit with a Wooden Sword, a zombie with an Iron Helmet must be hit with an Iron Sword, a zombie with a Gold Helmet must be hit with a Gold Sword, and a zombie with a diamond helmet must be hit with a Diamond Sword.
### Test of Swiftness
Challenge Rules: 1. Temporary blocks will spawn to stand on. 2. If the block is red it'll disappear soon. 3. Don't stand around and fall into the lava!
The player spawns in a cage of barrier blocks, over a pool of lava. After a few seconds, the cage opens, and a block of green wool spawns. The player must stand on it. Green blocks keep on spawning, forming a snake-like pattern. After a block spawns, the previous one will turn orange, indicating it should not be stood on. After another block spawns, the orange block will turn red, indicating that it will disappear soon. After time, the task will be more difficult, with players having to jump 1- and 2-blocks across lava to reach the green wool.
### Test of Control
Challenge Rules: 1. Stare directly at a moving skeleton. 2. More accurate staring gives more points and time in the arena. 3. Don't look away for too long or fall in the lava!
### Test of Tenacity
Challenge Rules: 1. A ghast shoots fireballs at you. 2. Earn points by avoiding fireballs over time. 3. Don't trip and fall into lava!
The player spawns on a circular platform, with a ghast in one corner of the Arena area. The ghast will shoot fireballs at the player, which destroy the block they hit. After a certain amount of time, a message in chat will appear saying: The ghast is becoming more frustrated... Another ghast will spawn, and both will shoot fireballs at the player. After some more time, another ghast will spawn, and afterwards, another. The player will need to try and stay on the platform without falling into the lava for as long as possible.
## Milestones
Achieving a large number of points rewards the player with [[Belts|Belt]] upgrades and [[Combat|Combat XP]]. The [[Belts]] rewarded are [[Soulbound|Co-op Soulbound]].
|Milestone|Points; Achieved|Belt; Upgrade| [[Combat]]; Experience|
|-|-|-|-|
|Yellow Belt Upgrade|1,000|Yellow Belt ([[Belts]]) Upgrade|+80,000|
|Green Belt Upgrade|2,000|Green Belt ([[Belts]]) Upgrade|+100,000|
|Blue Belt Upgrade|4,000|Blue Belt ([[Belts]]) Upgrade|+120,000|
|Brown Belt Upgrade|6,000|Brown Belt ([[Belts]]) Upgrade|+140,000|
|Black Belt Upgrade|7,000|Black Belt ([[Belts]]) Upgrade|+160,000|

## Tips
- When doing the Test of Mastery, DO NOT ignore red targets. Shoot those as much as possible. This can be done by checking your ping and shooting your arrows accordingly. For example, if you have 100ms Ping, then try to shoot your arrows when the targets are 0.1 seconds away from turning red. It is recommended to use mods to know when the target turns red.
- There are 7 Tests and you need 7000 points to obtain the [[Black Belt]].
  - 1000 points on each Test can be done, however, Points past 1000 still counts. You can offset low score on Tests you are having trouble on with Tests where you are competent at.
## Trivia
- The background music for the Dojo is a note-block rendition of Club Penguin's dojo music.
- The swords in the Test of Control come pre-enchanted with [[Knockback (Enchantment)|Knockback III]], a normally unobtainable level.

# Dolphin Trainer
Categories: Locations
[Dolphin Trainer]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[West Village]]
Coordinates: -67 52 -96
The Dolphin Trainer is a sub-location of the [[West Village]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Once the player enters the Dolphin Trainer they will gain effects similar to that of a dolphin: they will glide through the water and when surfacing, will leap into the air.
At the end of the cave is an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] and a Teleport Pad leading Out! which when stepped on will teleport the player back to the [[West Village]].

# Dragon's Lair (Crystal Hollows)
Aliases: Dragon Lair, Dragon's Lair, Dragon's Lair (disambiguation), Dragons Lair, Golden Dragon (NPC), The Golden Dragon
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs, Crystal Hollows
[Dragon's Lair]
Type: [[Mining]] Location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Precise Location: Varies between lobbies
← Previous Location: [[Mithril Deposits]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Golden Dragon]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Gemstone]] Golden Dragon Egg ([[Golden Dragon Pet]])
The Dragon's Lair is the unofficial name of a location in the [[Crystal Hollows]] where players can buy the [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]]. It rarely spawns in the [[Mithril Deposits]].
When a player is getting closer to the Dragon's Lair, the sound of a dragon's wings flapping begins to play, occasionally accompanied by a roar.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Dragon's Lair:
|Image|NPC|Minimap|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Golden Dragon]]|[[Golden Dragon]]|Dragon's Lair; Moves Around|Dragon's Lair; Moves Around|Sells the [[Golden Dragon Pet]].|✔|
## Trivia
- Entering the Dragon's Lair for the first time awards the player with the [[Achievements|"How to train your dragon?" Achievement]].
- The Dragon's Lair the dragon itself and its first line of dialogue are a reference to Smaug from the The Hobbit.
- Purchasing a [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Egg]] award the player with the [[Achievements|"The Golden Dragon's Egg" Achievement]].
- Before the September 17th, 2026 update, only maximum of three [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon Eggs]] could be purchased in each lobby.
- The [[Golden Dragon Pet|Golden Dragon]] is one of few [[NPC|NPCs]] that can talk despite being blocks: other similar [[NPC|NPCs]] are the [[Jade Dragon]] and [[Oruo The Almighty]].

NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dragon's Nest
■ Summon the Ender Dragon!
■ Fight Zealots.
## Features
The Dragon's Nest features many attractions.
### Teleport Pads
There are several teleport pads found on different spikes and structures in the Dragon's Nest. They allow the player to warp between locations within the area.
|Name|Coordinates|
|-|-|
|Bedrock Point|-655, 26, -241 ([[Coordinates]])|
|The Peak|-670, 32, -208 ([[Coordinates]])|
|Obsidian Rock|-684, 18, -231 ([[Coordinates]])|
|End Spire|-720, 56, -229 ([[Coordinates]])|
|Endertower|-700, 18, -310 ([[Coordinates]])|
|Dragon's View|-713, 41, -317 ([[Coordinates]])|
### Dragon's Gate
The Dragon's Gate is the entrance to the Dragon's Nest. During [[Ender Dragon]] fights, the Gate closes, and players must deal damage to it for it to open. Entering through the Dragon's Gate will lead the player to a set of stairs, which lead to the floor of the Dragon's Nest. Underneath the stairs is the [[Void Slate]], containing various other features.
|Damage|Message|
|-|-|
|300,000|☬ The Dragon's Gate is weakening!|
|550,000|☬ The Dragon's Gate is starting to budge!|
|800,000|☬ The Dragon's Gate is nearly open!|
|1,000,000|☬ The Dragon's Gate has opened!|
### Dragon's Altar
The Dragon's Altar is a structure in the center of the Dragon's Nest. It features 8 [[End Portal Frame|End Portal Frames]]. Once the Dragon Egg spawns, players can place [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] in order to summon a [[Ender Dragon|Dragon]]. Once a [[Summoning Eye]] is placed, a [[Sleeping Eye]] will appear in the player's inventory, acting as a placeholder. Players who placed a [[Summoning Eye]] can retrieve it at any time, to revert the [[Sleeping Eye]] back to a [[Summoning Eye]], unless a [[Ender Dragon|Dragon]] has already been spawned.
Once 8 [[Summoning Eye|Summoning Eyes]] are placed, a beam goes up along the glass, and the Dragon Egg hatches to summon a dragon, disappearing when the boss is spawned. After hatching, the Dragon Egg structure is destroyed, preventing another dragon from being summoned. The Dragon Egg respawns a minute after the Ender Dragon is defeated, allowing players to summon another dragon.
|Placement|Message|
|-|-|
|1/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (1/8)|
|2/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (2/8)|
|3/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (3/8)|
|4/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (4/8)|
|5/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (5/8)|
|6/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (6/8)|
|7/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! (7/8)|
|8/8|☬ [player] placed a Summoning Eye! Brace yourselves! (8/8)|

# Dreadfarm
Categories: Locations
[Dreadfarm]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])

Next Location →: [[Village Plaza]] [[Déjà Vu Alley]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Chicken (NPC)]] [[Cow (NPC)]] [[Shania (Rift)]] [[Sheep (NPC)]] [[Sick Farmer]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): Chicken ([[Chicken (Rift)]]) Not Chicken ([[Chicken (Rift)]]) [[Volt]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Metaphoric Egg]] [[Volt Crux]]
Resources: [[Agaricus Cap]] [[Caducous Stem]] [[Wilted Berberis]]
[Metadata]
ID: DREADFARM
The Dreadfarm is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Farm]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Dreadfarm will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Enigma: Farm Balloons
⇨ Enigma: Farm Flower Pot
⇨ Enigma: Beanstalk
⇨ Reed's Boat: Replacement Wood
⇨ Eye: The Baba Yaga
⇨ Harvest for Shania
⇨ Montezuma's Fifth
Enigma Souls: 4/4
## Features
The Dreadfarm features many attractions. The player must have secured the [[Supreme Timecharm]] in order to access the location.
### Crops
The Dreadfarm has multiple small plots, where a variety of plants grow. All of the crops must be harvested with a [[Wand of Farming]] to yield items.
Looking at the Brown Mushrooms on the ground for a few seconds will make them briefly turn red, at which point the player can harvest them to yield an [[Agaricus Cap]]. Additionally, clicking the Rose Bushes from top to bottom will drop [[Caducous Stem|Caducous Stems]], and breaking any of the Dead Bushes surrounded with purple particles give [[Wilted Berberis]].
### Balloons
Floating above a chest at one end of the Dreadfarm is a large bunch of balloons. The player must shoot each and every one of them with a [[Berberis Blowgun]] to drop the [[Anvil]] keeping them in the air, which will unlock the chest and reveal an Enigma Soul.
### The Baba Yaga
One of [[Porhtal]]'s eyes, The Baba Yaga, can be found in the Dreadfarm. The eye's condition is ROGUE, and can be defeated to gain a permanent +30 [[Rift Time]] and the ability to instantly teleport to it from [[Porhtal]] or any of their other eyes. Teleporting to The Baba Yaga costs 7m30s [[Rift Time]] and can only be done if the player has enough time remaining.
### Montezuma Cat Soul
Located behind a [[Cracked Wall|weak wall]] made of cracked stone bricks, the player can find the fifth [[Montezuma Pet|Montezuma Soul Piece]], which is located at [[Coordinates|-24, 72, -198]]. The wall can be broken down using a [[Horsezooka]], and finding the soul piece will add an additional +15 [[Rift Time]] to the [[Montezuma Pet|Pet]].

# Dungeon Hub
Aliases: Dungeons Hub
Categories: Locations, Dungeons, Hidden Locations
[Dungeon Hub]
Dungeon_Hub.png The Dungeon Hub as seen in game.
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 7
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Adventurer Saul]] [[Crag]] [[Croesus]] [[Dungeon Hub Selector]] [[Guildford]] [[Malik]] [[Mort]] [[Ophelia]] [[Vulcan]]
[Metadata]
ID: DUNGEON_HUB
The Dungeon Hub is a [[Locations|Location]] accessible through the [[Hub Island]] via a portal at [[Coordinates|-34, 72, 23]] or by using the [[Fast Travel]] feature in the SkyBlock Menu. This area has been made as a means to access all existing Dungeons and all Dungeon-related shops in one place.
Portal to Dungeon Hub in the [[Village]].
Damage (noticeably, [[Damage Calculation|Fall Damage]]) is disabled in the Dungeon Hub.
NOTE: The Fast Travel warp is not unlocked if the player has not gone through the Portal.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Dungeon Hub, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dungeon Hub
■ Join Dungeons!
■ Meet new friends and organize Dungeon parties!
■ Talk with Ophelia and Malik.
These are the additional tasks:
- Play with Friends!
- Create your own custom party.
- Find [[Fairy Souls]] (7)
- Hub Racing (for [[Wither Essence]] and Speed Accessories. Located under the main dungeon area)
## Purpose
The main purpose of the Dungeon Hub is to queue into the [[Catacombs]] and [[Kuudra]]. [[Mort]] allows the player to queue into Dungeons through Party Finder; [[Malik]] allows the player to upgrade items with [[Essence]], dungeonize gear, apply [[Reforging|Reforges]], and salvage items; and [[Ophelia]] serves as a shop for lots of convenient Dungeon items and powerful gear, like the [[Wither Goggles]], [[Giant Cleaver]], [[Death Bow]], [[Steel Chestplate]], and [[Mender Crown]].
[[Crag]] allows the player to access [[Kuudra]], and [[Vulcan]] provides a shop for [[Crimson Essence]]. [[Vesuvius]] can also be found in the [[Forgotten Skull]], who serves a similar purpose to Croseus.
[[Croesus]] also allows the player to open unclaimed [[Dungeon Reward Chest|Dungeon Reward Chests]] and [[Kuudra|Kuudra Chests]] for 72 hours, and completing [[The Catacombs - Floor III]] allows the player to access the Catacombs without a party by giving [[Saul's Recommendation]] to Mort.
## Hub Racing
Players can do the Hub Racing quest by talking with [[Guildford]] and selecting which destination and type of race.
There are 3 different race routes:
- Crystal Core
- Giant Mushroom
- Precursor Ruins
Each route has 4 types of race:
- Anything (can use Abilities (like [[Aspect of the End]]/ [[Aspect of the Void|Void]], [[Leaping Sword|Leaping]]/ [[Silk-Edge Sword]]), [[Ender Pearl|Ender Pearls]], and [[Grappling Hook]])
- No Pearls (can use Abilities as stated above, but no Ender Pearls}}
- No Abilities (can use only the Grappling Hook)
- Nothing at all (can't use anything, and walking speed is capped at 130)
The player can choose whether or not they want to race with runback, where they must reach the goal and then run back to the start. With no runback they will be teleported back to Guildford after reaching the goal. In total, there are 24 different Dungeon Hub races.
For every race the player completes in time, Guildford will give 3/4/5/6 [[Wither Essence]] or a [[Cat Talisman]] Upgrade.
Players can obtain the Speed Accessories by completing (4/4) objectives in at least 1 category in a location. For the rewards, the order of the races doesn't matter.
For example, completing 4/4 objectives on Precursor Ruins + Anything + Runback would reward a [[Cat Talisman]], then completing 4/4 objectives on Precursor Ruins + Nothing at all + No Runback afterwards would reward a [[Lynx Talisman]].
Each reward upgrades the previous:
1. [[Cat Talisman]] - +1 [[Speed]] [[Rarity|UNCOMMON]] Accessory
2. [[Lynx Talisman]] - +2 [[Speed]] [[Rarity|RARE]] Accessory
3. [[Cheetah Talisman]] - +3 [[Speed]] [[Rarity|EPIC]] Accessory

[Metadata]
ID: MINING_3(ISLAND); DEEPER_CAVERNS(ZONE)
The Dwarven Mines is a location that is focused on [[Mining]]. It has several unique [[Dwarven Metals]], including [[Mithril]] and [[Titanium]]: the [[Heart of the Mountain]] is also unlocked here.
[[Mining|Mining XII (12)]] and completion of [[Rhys]]' quest is required to access the Dwarven Mines.
## Overview
The primary focuses of the Dwarven Mines are to complete [[Commissions]], which are tasks given by the [[King]], to level up your [[Heart of the Mountain]], collect better tools via [[The Forge]], and collect different varieties of [[Powder (disambiguation)|Powder]]: those being ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]], and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Dwarven Mines, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Dwarven Mines
■ Mine rare ores.
## Layout
Players will arrive in the [[Dwarven Village]], where [[Rhys]] can be found, as well as several commodities: [[Bubu]] sells pickaxes with various stats, [[Bulvar]] sells Tier 12 mining minions, [[Banker Broadjaw]] allows players to access their Bank, [[Lumina]] sells various light sources, [[Fragilis]] shows the Handy Block Guide, and [[Geo]] allows players to add [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] to their items.
The gate to the [[Crystal Hollows]] can be found at [[Gwendolyn]], who can be found at the outskirts of the Dwarven Village: after reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain IV (4)]], the player can pay a [[Coins]] fee to enter the Crystal Hollows for 6 hours.
Exiting the Dwarven Village will reveal a crossroads: the left path leads to the [[Royal Palace]] via the [[Palace Bridge]], which overlooks the [[Royal Mines]]; the right path leads to the rest of the Dwarven Mines via the [[Gates to the Mines]], which overlooks [[The Forge]].
Beyond the [[Great Ice Wall]], the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] can be found: it is inaccessible without [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] and completion of [[Dulin]]'s quest, which also grants permanent free entry to the [[Crystal Hollows]].
The [[Station Master]] can be found in the [[Dwarven Village]], with several [[Ticket Master|Ticket Masters]] found around stations in the Dwarven Mines. After paying a small [[Coins]] fee, depending on your proximity to your desired location, players can enter a minecart and travel to other areas around the Dwarven Mines.
## Events
Events will regularly happen around the Dwarven Mines every 20 minutes: they are either Active, Passive, or Random.
|Name|Type|Description|
|-|-|-|
|[[2x Powder]]|Passive|While is event is active, all ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]], ᠅ [[Gemstone Powder]], and ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] obtained will be doubled.|
|[[Better Together]]|Passive|While this event is active, players gain increased [[Mining Speed]] and [[Mining Fortune]] when they are in the same zone as another player, up to 5 players.|
|[[Mithril Gourmand]]|Active|While this event is active, [[Don Expresso]] appears in the [[Dwarven Tavern]]. Mining [[Mithril]] in a specified area has a chance to drop [[Tasty Mithril]]: 200 of this must be given to Don to complete the event, and giving at least 10 personally will grant extra rewards.|
|[[Raffle]]|Active|While this event is active, a Raffle Box appears in a random location: Raffle Tickets ([[Raffle Ticket]]) will also spawn in the zone, and must be handed in to the Raffle Box before the event ends. The more tickets you deposit, the higher the chance of you winning the raffle and gaining increased rewards.|
|[[Goblin Raid]]|Active|While this event is active, several hordes of Goblins ([[Goblin (disambiguation)]]) will appear in a specified location and must be defeated within 5 minutes for participants to claim rewards.|
|Fallen Star ([[Fallen Stars]])|Random|A Fallen Star ([[Fallen Stars]]) will randomly fall in a random location: [[Mithril]], [[Titanium]], and ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]] drops are increased, [[Starfall]] has a chance to be obtained, and Star Sentries ([[Star Sentry]]) will spawn.|
|[[Powder Ghast]]|Random|A [[Powder Ghast]] will appear in a random location and must be shot several times in order to be killed. Each successful shot grants ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]].|

## Trivia
- The map for the Dwarven Mines is internally called the Deeper Caverns.
- The Dwarven Mines were possibly initially called the Dwarven Caves. Trying to use the [[Royal Pigeon]] outside of the Dwarven Mines would previously display the message, "You may not use this Item outside the Dwarven Caves!".
- There used to be a hole in the ceiling (located at 66, 222, -83) that would lead the player onto the roof of the mines, where there was text, written in blocks, that read:
  - "WHY ARE YOU UP HERE?" "COS" "THE NERVE!"
- Before November 8, 2021, players could use the command /warpforge to travel to [[The Forge]], without having to consume a [[Travel Scrolls|Travel Scroll]]. Since November 8, 2021, a Travel Scroll must be consumed in order for the command to function. Prior to the update, the command could also be used while combat tagged or as a spectator in Dungeons.

# Dwarven Tavern
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines
[Dwarven Tavern]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Music: "Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Village]]
Coordinates: 25 202 -134
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Bomin]] [[Brynmor]] [[Gimley]] [[Hornum]] [[Sargwyn]] [[Tarwen]]
Dwarven Tavern is a location inside the [[Dwarven Village]]. This location has 5 NPCs: four drunks ( [[Gimley]], [[Hornum]], [[Tarwen]], and [[Sargwyn]]) and [[Brynmor]], the bartender.
## Purpose
The tavern is a two-storied building, although only the first floor is accessible. Four tables can be found inside, and three outside: the aforementioned NPCs sit inside the tavern. Talking to them and replying with "burp" will reward a [[Dwarven Tankard]].
[[Brynmor]] sells the [[Powder Pie]], [[Filet O' Fortune]], and [[Chilled Pristine Potato]], each of which require higher [[Heart of the Mountain|HotM]] levels to purchase.

# Déjà Vu Alley
Aliases: Deja Vu Alley
Categories: Locations
[Déjà Vu Alley]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Coordinates: 11 68 -105
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Blacksmith (Rift)]] [[Detective Amog]]
[Metadata]
ID: DEJA_VU_ALLEY
The Déjà Vu Alley is a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It leads to the [[Living Cave]].
## Features
The Déjà Vu Alley features a few attractions.
### Red Light, Green Light
The Déjà Vu Alley is filled with particles and blocks of a certain color. Every few seconds, starting off with green, all colored blocks and particles in the Alley will turn to a different color. When it is green, players are safe to walk. Soon after, the Alley will turn yellow, indicating that soon it will turn red. If the player enters or moves (including camera movements) inside the Déjà Vu Alley whilst it is red, they will be flung up into the air in the [[Village Plaza]] and 100 [[Rift Time]] will be subtracted from their time remaining.
### Reverse-Murder Investigation
After purchasing the [[Tactician's Murder Weapon]] from [[Master Tactician Fink]], [[Detective Amog]] will appear in one of the houses in the Déjà Vu Alley, next to the [[Blacksmith (Rift)|Blacksmith]]. After talking to the [[Blacksmith (Rift)|Blacksmith]], the player must go back to the [[Murder House]] in order to search for more clues to progress in the quest.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Déjà Vu Alley:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Blacksmith (Rift)]]|[[Blacksmith (Rift)]]|Déjà Vu Alley; -1.5, 68, -142 ([[Coordinates]])|Plays a role in [[Detective Amog]]'s reverse-murder quest.|✕|
|[[Detective Amog]]|[[Detective Amog]]|Déjà Vu Alley; 2, 68, -139.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during his first reverse-murder quest.|✕|
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Déjà Vu Alley.|

# Einary's Emporium
Categories: Locations, Events, Season of Jerry
[Einary's Emporium]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: -15 76 63
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Einary]]
[Metadata]
ID: EINARYS_EMPORIUM
Einary's Emporium is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Einary's Emporium, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Einary's Emporium
■ Talk to Einary.
■ Discover unique winter-themed rewards.
■ Spend your hard-earned North Stars.
## Features
Einary's Emporium features one main attraction.
### Einary
[[Einary]] is an [[NPC]] located on the ground floor of Einary's Emporium. They own a shop that the player can open, which features a [[Reindeer Pet]], Tier XII [[Minions]], the [[Snow Suit Armor|Snow Suit Equipment]], [[Refrigerate|Refrigerate I]], [[Magic Top Hat]], [[Winter Fragment|Winter Fragments]], [[Luck|Luck VI]], [[Looting|Looting IV]], and [[Scavenger|Scavenger IV]]. They also sell the [[White Gift Talisman]] line, which is exclusive for purchase during the month of December.
## Trivia
- On the top floor of Einary's Emporium, there is an [[Abiphones|Abiphone X Blue]] on the floor.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Nov 30, 2022|Added Einary's Emporium.|

# Election Room
Categories: Locations, Events, Mayor Election
[Election Room]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Community Center]]
Coordinates: 6 48 41
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Aatrox]] [[Candidate Jerry]] [[Clerk Seraphine]] [[Cole]] [[Derpy]] [[Diana]] [[Diaz]] [[Finnegan]] [[Foxy]] [[Marina]] [[Paul]] [[Scorpius]]
The Election Room is a location in which players can talk to and vote for mayor candidates.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Election Room, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Election Room
■ Vote in the Mayor Election.
## Purpose
The Election Room is only available during the [[Mayor Election]] period, during which players can take a teleport pad from the community center to the room. Five candidates will be in the room. When a mayor is elected, the election is closed the teleport pad will be replaced with a locked door, and players may not enter the Election Room.
## NPCs
There are 12 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Election Room:

|[[Aatrox]]|[[Aatrox]]|Election Room; -3.5, 49, 34.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here when elected as Mayor ([[Mayor Election]]). Also appears on an outer stand when running to become Mayor or Minister ([[Mayor Election]]).|✕|

## Features
The Empty Bank features a few attractions.
### Barter Bank Show
Whilst on the [[Alpha Hypixel Network]], the Empty Bank hosted the [[Barter Bank Show]], which involved a currency called NotCoins and selecting chests.
### Hologram
In the basement of the Empty Bank is a hologram, located in the center of the room, and floating in the air. It reads:
THERE'S NOTHING HERE!
Where did all the coins go?

# Enigma's Crib
Categories: Locations
[Enigma's Crib]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Wyld Woods]]
Coordinates: -41 71 93
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Enigma]]
[Metadata]
ID: ENIGMA
Enigma's Crib is a sub-location of the [[Wyld Woods]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
### Enigma
[[Enigma]] is the sole [[NPC]] located in Enigma's Crib. Whenever the player turns their back to them or directs their line of vision in a direction other than [[Enigma]], they will change location quickly. Every 4 [[Enigma Souls]] the player finds, they can exchange them with [[Enigma]] for a price in order to gain a small but permanent stat bonus.
### Orbs
Unlike the orbs that can be picked up around the [[Rift Dimension|Rift]] to grant [[Rift Time]] and [[Motes]], the orbs in Enigma's Crib grant only 10 [[Motes]] as opposed to the 25 granted by ordinary orbs. Additionally, the distance requirement for orbs to spawn is removed, and multiple can spawn at the same time in a small radius.
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in Enigma's Crib:
|Image|NPC|Minimap|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Enigma]]|[[Enigma]]|Enigma's Crib; Moves Around|Enigma's Crib; Moves Around|Sells the [[Enigma Cloak]] and upgrades for it.|✔|
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in Enigma's Crib:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Next to Enigma|-26.5|71|90.5|Located in the pool of water.|

# Fairy Grotto
Aliases: Grotto
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows
[Fairy Grotto]
Type: [[Mining]] location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])
Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Precise Location: Varies
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Butterfly]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: Jasper Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) Rough Jasper Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
Resources: [[Hard Stone]] Jasper ([[Gemstone]]) Jasper Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]])
The Fairy Grotto is a [[Locations|Location]] that rarely spawns in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. It is one of the only two sources of [[Gemstone|Jasper]], the other being the [[Glacite Mineshafts]]: players can also find the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jasper Crystal]] in [[Treasure Chest|Treasure Chests]] or from killing [[Butterfly|Butterflies]].
Up to 3 Fairy Grottos can spawn in a [[Crystal Hollows]] lobby.
There is a 1/200 chance of fishing up a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] from water in this location. This can be done twice.
## Bugs
- Some Fairy Grottos do not say Fairy Grotto on the scoreboard, and it is not possible to fish up [[Fairy Souls]] in them.
## History

# Far Reserve
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines, Mining
[Far Reserve]

The Far Reserve is a section of the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It connects to the [[Rampart's Quarry]], [[Upper Mines]], and [[Goblin Burrows]].
## Overview
The Far Reserve, similar to most other mining area in the Dwarven Mines, consists largely of veins of [[Mithril]] and several wooden platforms that allow the player to reach higher levels of the area.
A [[Ticket Master]] can be found in the Far Reserve. The [[Raffle]] event can occur here, as well as [[Fallen Stars]].

# Farm
Categories: Locations
[Farm]
Type: [[Farming]] location
Sub-locations: [[Communal Stew]] [[Farmhouse]]
Fairy Souls: 6
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: 65 71 -82
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Alchemist]] [[Anita]] [[Arthur]] [[Farm Merchant]] [[Farmer Rigby]] [[Feast Baker Scott]] [[Feast Chef Ted]] [[Jacob]] [[Ludleth]] [[Shania]] [[Tyashoi Alchemist]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Wheat]] [[Seeds]] [[Milk Bucket]]
The Farm is an area for the player to obtain [[Farming]] levels early in the game. It can be found north-east of the [[Village]] on the [[Hub Island]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Farm, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Farm
■ Learn about the Farming Skill at Farmer Rigby.
■ Gather Wheat.
■ Learn about Minion Upgrades at Arthur.
■ Travel to The Barn.
## Purpose
The Farm includes five different [[Wheat]] farming areas though this is the only [[Crops (disambiguation)|crop]] available to the player. The wheat is constantly regrown by wheat [[Floating Crystals|crystals]] above them. A [[Wheat Minion]] is on display, and its inventory can be accessed and looted. It always holds an [[Enchanted Bread]], a [[Budget Hopper]], and a [[Compactor]]. The NPC nearby, [[Arthur]], explains certain things about [[Minions]]. A small shack contains the cow [[Shania]] and [[Farmer Rigby]], who will request wheat from the player. Left-clicking Shania with a [[Bucket]] allows you to obtain the [[Magical Milk Bucket]]. Right-clicking gives regular milk. There is a launchpad leading to [[The Barn]].
## NPCs
There are 11 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Farm:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Alchemist]]|[[Alchemist]]|Farm; 80.5, 72, -90.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells items related to [[Alchemy]] and Brewing ([[Potions]]).|✔|
|[[Anita]]|[[Anita]]|[[Farmhouse]]; 53.5, 73, -128.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various [[Farming]] items in exchange for Jacob's Tickets ([[Jacob's Ticket]]) and [[Medals]].|✔|
|[[Arthur]]|[[Arthur]]|Farm; 53.5, 72, -111.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Owns a [[Wheat Minion]] and talks about [[Minions]] and [[Minion Upgrades]] that can be exist.|✕|
|[[Farm Merchant]]|[[Farm Merchant]]|Farm; 63.5, 72, -113.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various [[Farming]]-related items, many of which are [[Collections]].|✔|
|[[Farmer Rigby]]|[[Farmer Rigby]]|Farm; 61.5, 72, -147.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Rewards the player for feeding cow, [[Shania]].|✕|
|[[Feast Baker Scott]]|[[Feast Baker Scott]]|[[Communal Stew]]; 77.5, 72, -83.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells [[Farming]]-related items in exchange for [[Kernels]] while [[Finnegan]]'s Grand Feast is active.|✔|
|[[Feast Chef Ted]]|[[Feast Chef Ted]]|[[Communal Stew]]; 84.5, 72, -78.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to the [[Harvest Feast]] and Grand Feast ([[Harvest Feast]]) events, and which crops are in-season as part of it.|✕|
|[[Jacob]]|[[Jacob]]|[[Farmhouse]]; 46.5, 73, -128.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Hosts Jacob's Farming Contests ([[Jacob's Farming Contest]]).|✕|
|[[Ludleth]]|[[Ludleth]]|Farm; 81.5, 59, -112.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells about [[Rose Dragon Pet]] after the player has completed their offer on [[The Garden]].|✔|
|[[Shania]]|[[Shania]]|Farm; 59.5, 72, -144.5 ([[Coordinates]])|The cow of [[Farmer Rigby]].|✕|
|[[Tyashoi Alchemist]]|[[Tyashoi Alchemist]]|Farm; 70.5, 71, -85.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears during the Great Spook ([[The Great Spook]]) to sell items.|✔|
## Fairy Souls
There are 6 [[Fairy Souls]] found in the Farm:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Location|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|26|77|102|-88|Farm|Alchemist's Spire|✔|
|27|82|97|-139|Farm|Windmill|✔|
|28|87|68|-154|Farm|Under Farming Islands Jump-pad|✔|
|29|136|74|-105|Farm|Chicken Coop|✔|
|30|61|80|-139|Farm|2nd Floor of Barn|✔|
|31|70|73|-106|Farm|By Netherwart|✔|
## Tips
It is recommended that you farm here for [[Wheat]] rather than at [[The Barn]], due to large wheat fields that are rarely looted by players and regrow quickly.
## Trivia
- The [[Wheat Minion]] in the Farm can be looted, but the upgrades and the fuel cannot be taken out. Trying to do so will show a message in the chat: [NPC] Arthur: Sorry but that's mine!
- Interestingly, this minion can be upgraded, however the upgrade gets reverted when the server restarts.

The Fisherman's Hut is a hut located within the [[Fishing Outpost]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Fisherman's Hut, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Fisherman's Hut
■ Learn about the Sea Creature Guide from Gavin.
■ Use the Ship Navigator to reach the Backwater Bayou!
## Features
The Fisherman's Hut is home to [[Gavin]], who talks about the [[Sea Creatures|Sea Creature Guide]]. [[Captain Baha]] is also located behind the Fisherman's Hut who helps the player to travel to the [[Backwater Bayou]].

The Flower House is a [[Locations|Location]] on the outskirts of the [[Village]] on the [[Hub Island]]. It serves as home to [[Marco]] and an automatically regrowing field of [[Flowers]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Flower House, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Flower House
■ Talk to Marco about Spray Cans.
■ Gather flowers.
## Purpose
The Flower House is home to [[Marco]], who first tasks the player to bring back 5 [[Rose Red]] dyes. These can be collected by harvesting [[Flowers|Poppies]] in the [[Flowers|flower]] field outside the house. Upon providing these items, Marco will provide a [[Spray Can]].

|[[Marco]]|[[Marco]]|Flower House; 90.5, 76, 3.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Tasks the player with giving them 5 Poppy ([[Flowers]]), rewarding the [[Spray Can]] for doing so.|✕|

The Forest is a location that is west of spawn on the [[Hub Island]], with Oak trees that players can chop down. It contains the [[Foraging Camp]], where the player is introduced to [[Foraging]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Forest, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Forest
## Purpose
The Forest serves as a place to harvest [[Oak Log]], [[Leaves|Oak Leaves]], [[Saplings|Oak Saplings]], and [[Apple|Apples]].
[[The Park]] lies to the west of the Forest (stepping on the [[Launch Pad]]) and the [[Graveyard]] is north of the Forest.

# Forest Temple
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs
[Forest Temple]
Type: [[Foraging]] location
Level: Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]])
Music: "Moonglade Marsh ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Moonglade Marsh]]
Coordinates: -636 60 75
← Previous Location: [[Ancient Ruins]]
Next Location →: None
[Metadata]
ID: FOREST_TEMPLE
The Forest Temple is a sub-location of the [[Murkwater Depths]]. [[Ancient Ruins]] lantern puzzle must be completed to enter.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Forest Temple, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Forest Temple
■ The Altar of an ancient being.
## Features
The Forest Temple is an underwater temple where players can donate [[Coins]] and [[Enchanted Fig Log|Enchanted Fig Logs]] to gain [[Foraging Fortune]] and become a temporary benefactor.
### Puzzle
The player must rotate cyan glazed terracotta on the floor until it matches the pattern on the wall. Solving the Forest Temple's puzzle reactivates the temple grants [[Foraging Fortune]] based on how many players have also reactivated the temple.
### Bonuses
The player can donate 70,000 [[Coins]] and 2 [[Enchanted Fig Log|Enchanted Fig Logs]] to become a Benefactor for one real-life day. This doubles the [[Foraging Fortune]] gained and makes thrown [[Axes]] 25% larger and faster. The player will lose their status as a Benefactor of the [[Desert Temple]] if they were one, however, the time that this donation would have lasted will not be lost and rather added to the time as Forest Temple Benefactor.
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 5, 2025|Added Forest Temple.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 23, 2025|0.23|Added Forest Temple.|

Galatea is an archipelago of [[Foraging]] islands, including the [[Moonglade Marsh]] and [[Torrhus Canyon]], as well as a third planned island known as [[Lunarise]], which is currently unreleased. It can be accessed from [[The Park]] after reaching [[Foraging|Foraging XII (12)]].
## Purpose
Galatea, being a [[Foraging]] island, has several Foraging-related mechanics. These include the [[Heart of the Forest]], [[Tree Gifts]], Tree Styles, [[Sweep]], and [[Hunting]].
### Heart of the Forest
The [[Heart of the Forest]] is a skill tree that grants various perks related to [[Foraging]] and [[Hunting]]. One can obtain HotF EXP through [[Tree Gifts]] and [[Agatha|Agatha Contests]], and leveling up the Heart of the Forest or Center of the Forest grants Token of the Forest.
### Tree Gifts
Tree Gifts are a bundle of loot obtained from fully cutting down a [[Fig Log|Fig]], [[Mangrove Log|Mangrove]], or [[Helix Log|Helix]] tree. Rewards obtained scale based on participation up to all rewards received at 33% of the tree broken: as a result, this encourages working together to chop down trees.
### Tree Styles
Tree Styles dictate the order in which you cut down different parts of a tree in. Different trees have different [[Toughness]], which can be bypassed by having more [[Sweep]] than a tree's toughness. Failing to cut down a tree in the correct order results in a 50% less logs broken.
|Tree|Location|Toughness|Style|
|-|-|-|-|
|Fig Tree ([[Fig Log]])|Upper area of the [[Moonglade Marsh]]|10 [[Toughness]]|Trunk → Branches|
|Mangrove Tree ([[Mangrove Log]])|[[Murkwater Loch]]|50 [[Toughness]]|Branches → Trunk → Roots|
|Helix Tree ([[Helix Log]])|[[Torrhus Canyon]]|150 [[Toughness]]|Beige Logs → Red Logs|
## Zones
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Moonglade Marsh]]|The first island of Galatea. Requires Foraging XII (12) ([[Foraging]]) to access.|None|
|[[Tangleburg]]|A village near the front of the island, where one can learn the basics of [[Foraging]] and [[Hunting]].|[[Albert]], [[David Hunterborough]], [[Oden]], [[Pounce]], [[Salvage Old Attributes]], [[Swoop]], [[Talia]]|
|^ [[Fusion House]]|A place where players can fuse Attribute Shards ([[Attributes]]) through the Fusion Machine ([[Attribute Fusion]]).|[[Kysha]]|

|^ [[Tangleburg Bank]]|A bank that allows players to access the Banking System ([[Bank]]).|[[Banker Bonsai]]|
|^ [[Tangleburg Library]]|A library housing [[Loras]], who talks about the Precursors.|[[Loras]]|
|^ [[Tangleburg's Path]]|A path just outside [[Tangleburg]] leading to [[The Park]], branching off into a small pond.|[[Hina]]|
|[[Evergreen Plateau]]|A large plateau where players first learn how to use [[Lassos]] and Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Forest Creatures. Several large Fig Trees can be found here, where players can forage for Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|[[Auryon]]|
|[[South Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]). The [[Moonglade Beacon]] and [[Trial of Blue Flames]] can be found here.|[[Boris]], [[Brian]], [[Sawyer]], [[Soul Campfire Adept]], [[Soul Campfire Initiate]]|
|[[South Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tomb Floodway]].|None|
|[[West Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|
|[[Verdant Summit]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|None|
|[[Westbound Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|None|
|[[Murkwater Outpost]]|A place where players can trade [[Forest Essence]] for various [[Foraging]], [[Hunting]], and other miscellaneous perks.|[[Rosemary]]|
|[[North Reaches]]|A place where players can forage Fig Logs ([[Fig Log]]) and Lushlilacs ([[Lushlilac]]).|[[Alan]], [[Kiara]], [[Teddy]], [[Vaeri]]|
|[[North Wetlands]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]). Houses the [[Red House]] and leads to the [[Side-Ember Way]].|None|
|^ [[Red House]]|A place where players can donate different variants of The Fish ([[The Fish Family]]) to [[Coral]] in exchange for [[Fishing]] XP, [[Respiration]], and SkyBlock XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]]).|[[Coral]]|
|[[Moonglade's Edge]]|A place where players can forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and learn how to use [[Huntraps]]. Houses a portal to the [[Torrhus Canyon]]. Also leads to the [[Tranquil Pass]].|[[Dax]]|
|[[Murkwater Loch]]|The center of the Moonglade Marsh, where players can learn about Agatha's Contests ([[Agatha]]) and forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]).|[[Agatha]], [[Jaeger]]|
|[[Murkwater Shallows]]|A place where players can fish, forage Mangrove Logs ([[Mangrove Log]]) and [[Sea Lumies]].|[[Nemo]]|
|[[Murkwater Depths]]|An underwater area where players can collect [[Sea Lumies]] and Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Water Creatures. Branches off into the [[Forest Temple]] via the [[Ancient Ruins]], [[Drowned Reliquary]] via the [[Reefguard Pass]], the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]] via the [[Dive-Ember Pass]], the [[Driptoad Delve]] via the [[Driptoad Pass]], the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]], the [[Squid Cave]], and the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]).|None|
|[[Ancient Ruins]]|An underwater entrance to the [[Forest Temple]]. Punch the soul lanterns in ascending order of number of [[Sea Lumies]] next to it to open the gate.|[[Tilus]]|
|[[Forest Temple]]|Donate [[Coins]] and Enchanted Fig Logs ([[Enchanted Fig Log]]) to the Forest Starborn to become a benefactor and gain [[Foraging Fortune]].|None|
|[[Reefguard Pass]]|An underwater path to access the [[Drowned Reliquary]].|None|
|[[Drowned Reliquary]]|An underwater ruin containing Tidetots ([[Tidetot]]), Seacurses ([[Seacurse]]), and Hydrospears ([[Hydrospear]]).|None|
|[[Squid Cave]]|An underwater area found on the path to the Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]]) that contains Lumisquids ([[Lumisquid]]).|None|
|Dragon's Lair ([[Dragon's Lair (Galatea)]])|A cavern where the [[Jade Dragon]] sells the Jade Dragon Egg ([[Jade Dragon Pet]]).|[[Jade Dragon]]|
|[[Dive-Ember Pass]]|An underwater path to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Stride-Ember Fissure]]|A lava lake where players can hunt Stridersurfers ([[Stridersurfer]]).|None|
|[[Side-Ember Way]]|A bypass that leads to the [[Stride-Ember Fissure]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Pass]]|An underwater path that connects the [[Driptoad Delve]] and the [[Murkwater Depths]].|None|
|[[Driptoad Delve]]|A pond where the player can fish and enter the [[Murkwater Depths]] from the [[Driptoad Pass]].|None|
|[[Kelpwoven Tunnels]]|An underwater tunnel full of coral where the player can hunt Spikes ([[Spike]]).|None|
|[[Bubbleboost Column]]|An underwater vertical tunnel to quickly travel to [[Tangleburg]] from the [[Kelpwoven Tunnels]] and [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]].|None|
|[[Wyrmgrove Tomb]]|A place where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Chillshots ([[Chillshot]]) and Chillblades ([[Chillblade]]).|None|
|[[Tomb Floodway]]|A place where players can fish up Flexbones ([[Flexbone]]).|[[Martin]]|
|[[Tranquil Pass]]|A tunnel where players can harvest [[Bamboo]].|None|
|[[Tranquility Sanctum]]|A cavern where players can Hunt ([[Hunting]]) Bambuleafs ([[Bambuleaf]]) between Spring and Summer, and Mochibears ([[Mochibear]]) between Autumn and Winter.|None|

|[[Pangolin Hideaway]]|Occasionally contains Pangolins ([[Pangolin]]).|None|
|[[Torrhus Heights]]|The top portion of the island, where players can harvest Helix Trees ([[Helix Log]]) or go [[Hunting]] for unique [[Critters]].|[[Forager Rowan]], [[Maxim]]|
|[[Spring Path]]|A pathway to the [[Torrhus Springs]].|[[Hunter Jean]]|
|[[Torrhus Springs]]|An area to go [[Fishing]] or swimming down the large water spring.|[[Dory]]|
|^ [[Spring Shallows]]|The top portion of the [[Torrhus Springs]], containing Solars ([[Solar]]).|None|
|^ [[Spring Depths]]|The bottom portion of the [[Torrhus Springs]], which requires more [[Respiration]] and [[Pressure Resistance]] to survive through. Contains Water Snakes ([[Water Snake]]), Embers ([[Ember]]), and Sepialots ([[Sepialot]]). |^ [[Ant's Cave]]|Contains Ants ([[Ant]]) and [[Ant (NPC)]]. Accessed from a path in the [[Spring Depths]].|[[Ant (NPC)]]|
|^ [[Hotspot Haven]]|Contains a Fishing Hotspot ([[Fishing Hotspots]]) that lets players fish up the the [[Giant Water Bug Shard]]. Accessed from a path in the [[Spring Depths]].|[[Hunter McNeil]]|
|^ [[Desert Temple]]|Accessed at the bottom of the [[Spring Depths]] and after completing a color puzzle.|None|

# Gary's Shack
Categories: Locations, Events, Season of Jerry
[Gary's Shack]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
Coordinates: 55 103 56
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Gary]]
[Metadata]
ID: GARYS_SHACK
Gary's Shack is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Gary's Shack, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Gary's Shack
■ Talk to Gary.
■ Admire his snow machine.
## Purpose
[[Gary]] is an [[NPC]] located inside Gary's Shack. They grant dialogue under different conditions, and when [[Jerry's Workshop]] is under attack and has already lost enough [[Gifts]], [[Gary]] will power his snow cannon, and will shoot at the [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]].
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in Gary's Shack:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Gary]]|[[Gary]]|Gary's Shack; 53.5, 103, 56.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about their love for snow.|✕|
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Dec 18, 2019|0.7.5|Added Gary's Shack.|
|Nov 30, 2022|Added Gary's Shack as a Zone.|

# Gates to the Mines
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines
[Gates to the Mines]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])
Music: "Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 4 182 -60
The Gates to the Mines is a [[Locations|Location]] that connects the area past the [[Dwarven Village]] to [[The Forge]] and [[Rampart's Quarry]].
## Purpose
The Gates to the Mines connects to [[The Forge]] and [[Rampart's Quarry]].
## Trivia
- The Gates to the Mines used to contain a [[Ticket Master]] and the chest from [[Bednom]]'s quest.

[Glacial Cave]

ID: GLACIAL_CAVE
The Glacial Cave is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Glacial Cave, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Glacial Cave
■ Collect Ice without the threat of a watery grave.
■ Collect long lost buried items!
## Features
Glacial Cave features multiple attractions.
### Penguins
Scattered throughout the Glacial Cave are penguins. They wield a [[Wooden Pickaxe]] and stand on the scaffold found across the caves. The penguins have idle dialogue and mention [[Hendrik]], who can be found in the [[Hot Springs]].
### Frozen Treasure
Anywhere between 1 and 4 Frozen Treasures spawn every 10 seconds at given locations within the ice in the Glacial Cave. The player can obtain Frozen Treasures by breaking the Ice Blocks they are inside of.
The message when player find a treasure is:
FROZEN TREASURE! You found <treasure>!

# Glacite Mineshafts
Aliases: Glacite Mineshaft, Mineshaft, Mineshafts, Shaft
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs
[Glacite Mineshafts]
Type: [[Mining]] location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 7
Music: "Dead Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
← Previous Location: [[Glacite Tunnels]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Glacite Bowman]] [[Glacite Caver]] [[Glacite Mage]] [[Glacite Mutt]] [[Littlefoot]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Enchanted Glacite]] Fine Aquamarine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Fine Citrine Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Fine Onyx Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Fine Peridot Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Glacite Amalgamation]] [[Glacite Chunk]] [[Glacite Jewel]] [[Littlefoot Fluff]]
Resources: [[Ice]] [[Glacite]] [[Umber]] [[Tungsten]] [[Gemstone]] [[Mithril]] [[Titanium]] [[Hard Stone]] Gold ([[Gold Ingot]]) [[Glacite Powder]] [[Suspicious Scrap]]
Glacite Mineshafts are instanced locations that can be accessed by mining in the [[Glacite Tunnels]].
## Spawning
Glacite Mineshafts can be accessed by breaking blocks in the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. The base chance of spawning a Glacite Mineshaft is 0.05% (1 in 2000): this chance can be increased with the Surveryor perk in the [[Heart of the Mountain]] at Tier 8.
Glacite Mineshafts are covered by [[Pity and RNG Meters|Pity]]:
- The counter starts from 2,000, decreasing according to the Block Quality of the block mined. Once the counter reaches 0, the next block is guaranteed to spawn a Glacite Mineshaft portal.
- If a block is mined from [[Mining Spread]] or [[Heart of the Mountain|Pickobolus]], its Block Quality is halved.
  - For example, if a player with 300 [[Mining Spread]] mines a [[Glacite]] block, 3 nearby ones will be indirectly mined. In total, the counter decreases by 10: once by 4 and thrice by 2.
- The counter determines the chance of a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal spawning on the next block mined: it is the inverse of the counter.
- However, on the tab list and in the Pity menu, the counter appears as starting from 0, increasing to 2000 in contrary to the mechanics initially explained on its introduction
  - For example, if the in-game Pity counter appears at 859, the actual value is 2000-859=1141. Thus, the probability of spawning a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal on the next block is 0,088% (1 in 1141).
  - For example, if the in-game Pity counter appears at 1995, the actual value is 2000-1995=5. Thus, the probability of spawning a [[Glacite Mineshafts|Glacite Mineshaft]] portal on the next block is 20% (1 in 5).
Block Qualities:
|"Block"|Block Quality|
|-|-|
|Mithril Ores ([[Block Strength]])|2|
|Titanium Ores ([[Titanium]])|8|
|Umber Ores ([[Umber]])|4|
|Tungsten Ores ([[Tungsten]])|4|
|Glacite Ores ([[Glacite]])|4|
|Ruby Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]); Sapphire ([[Gemstone]]), Jade ([[Gemstone]]), Amber ([[Gemstone]]), Amethyst Gemstones ([[Gemstone]]); Topaz Gemstones ([[Gemstone]])|8|
|Aquamarine ([[Gemstone]]), Peridot ([[Gemstone]]), Citrine ([[Gemstone]]), Onyx Gemstones ([[Onyx (disambiguation)]])|10|
|Other (such as [[Hard Stone]])|0|
Each Glacite Mineshaft corresponds to a specific resource (e.g. [[Umber]], [[Tungsten]], [[Gemstone|Ruby]], etc), with some containing their respective resource's [[Gemstone Crystals|Gemstone Crystal]]. Most blocks inside Glacite Mineshafts can be broken, similar to the [[Crystal Hollows]].
Upon opening a Glacite Mineshaft, a portal will spawn, alongside the following dialogue:
MINESHAFT! A mineshaft portal spawned nearby!
Attempting to enter the Glacite Mineshaft will open the following interface:
Players have 30s to enter a Glacite Mineshaft before it closes, if the player has entered a Glacite Mineshaft within the past 30s, they are prevented from joining the current instance and immediately despawn. Additionally, the player's pity does not increase for 1 minute, if they're in the same [[Dwarven Mines]] lobby as before entering the Mineshaft.
### Types
The base chance for each mineshaft type to spawn is 5.88% (1 in 17 mineshafts). If the final block mined that leads to a mineshaft spawning matches one of the mineshaft types, it raises the chance for that mineshaft type to spawn to 33.33% (1 in 3 mineshafts), and lowers the chance for others to 4.17% (1 in 24 mineshafts).
If a gemstone mineshaft that is eligible for a crystal is chosen, there is a 25% (1 in 4) chance to get the crystal variant. This can be increased by +0.5% per level using the [[Heart of the Mountain|Crystalline]] Heart of the Mountain perk, up to +25.5% at level 51 (with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]). If a crystal variant does not spawn, the non-crystal variants have an equal chance to spawn. The _2 variants generally have the biggest veins, followed by _1, and finally _C.
The [[Heart of the Mountain|Vanguard Seeker]] perk increases the weight of the Fairy mineshaft by +1% per level, up to +51% at level 51 (with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]]) – bringing the chance to 8.62% (1 in 11.6 mineshafts), and the chance for other mineshafts to 5.71% (1 in 17.51 mineshafts). If an eligible block is mined, the chance for a matching mineshaft is 33.33% (1 in 3 mineshafts), then the chance for a Fairy mineshaft is 4.38% (1 in 22.84 mineshafts), and the chance for other mineshafts is 3.81% (1 in 26.26 mineshafts) each.

## Events
A Glacite Mineshaft has a chance of activating a random mining event. This event lasts until all players have evacuated the mineshaft. The possible events are the following:
- [[2x Powder]]
- [[Better Together]]
- [[Fortunate Freezing]]
## Corpses
Frozen Corpses can spawn in any Glacite Mineshaft. Depending on the size, 1-3 corpses will spawn: an additional Frozen Corpse can spawn in with [[Heart of the Mountain|Dead Man's Chest]] (+0.5-25%) and [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse Milestones]] 2 and 5 (+10-20%), raising the maximum number of corpses up to 4.
Lapis Frozen Corpses do not require a key to obtain loot from them. Upon looting a Lapis Frozen Corpse 3-6 (uniform distribution with an average of 4.5 Rolls) of the below drops will be selected. With [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk Gifts from the Departed at level 100 + [[Drills|Drill]] with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] and level 6 [[Grandpa Wolf|Corpse Milestone]], there is a 40.2% chance to gain an additional drop (bringing the average to 4.902). There is also a 0.0004% (1 in 250,000) chance for each roll to be a [[Frostbitten Dye]] instead, which can be boosted by [[Vincent]] to be 2x or 3x higher.

Umber and Tungsten Frozen Corpses share the same loot table. The only functional difference between the two is which key is required to loot them. Umber Frozen Corpses require the use of an [[Umber Key]] to obtain loot from them. Tungsten Frozen Corpses require the use of a [[Tungsten Key]] to obtain loot from them. Upon looting a Umber or Tungsten Frozen Corpse 4-7 (uniform distribution with an average of 5.5 Rolls) of the below drops will be selected. With [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk Gifts from the Departed at level 100 + [[Drills|Drill]] with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] and level 6 [[Grandpa Wolf|Corpse Milestone]], there is a 40.2% chance to gain an additional drop (bringing the average to 5.902). There is also a 0.001% (1 in 100,000) chance for each roll to be a [[Frostbitten Dye]] instead, which can be boosted by [[Vincent]] to be 2x or 3x higher.

Vanguard Frozen Corpses require the use of a [[Skeleton Key]] to obtain loot from them and only appear in the Fairy Mineshaft variation of the [[Glacite Mineshafts]]. Upon looting a Vanguard Frozen Corpse 5-8 (uniform distribution with an average of 6.5 Rolls) of the below drops will be selected. With [[Heart of the Mountain]] perk Gifts from the Departed at level 100 + [[Drills|Drill]] with [[Blue Cheese Goblin Omelette]] and level 6 [[Grandpa Wolf|Corpse Milestone]], there is a 40.2% chance to gain an additional drop (bringing the average to 6.902). There is also a 0.01% (1 in 10,000) chance for each roll to be a [[Frostbitten Dye]] instead, which can be boosted by [[Vincent]] to be 2x or 3x higher.

## Gemstone Crystals
Rarely a mineshaft may contain a [[Gemstone Crystals|Gemstone Crystal]] which is used to forge Perfect [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], it can be one of the following: [[Gemstone Crystals|Ruby Crystal]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Jasper Crystal]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Opal Crystal]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Onyx Crystal]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Aquamarine Crystal]], [[Gemstone Crystals|Citrine Crystal]] or [[Gemstone Crystals|Peridot Crystal]]. Only the shaft's owner can collect the crystal.
## Leaving Bonus
Leaving a Glacite Mineshaft either through its entrance or with the [[Ascension Rope]] grants additional [[Glacite Powder]].
- 1,000 (15 stacks plus 40) [[Glacite Powder]] as a base reward
- 500 (7 stacks plus 52) [[Glacite Powder]] per [[Suspicious Scrap]] found
- 250 (3 stacks plus 58) [[Glacite Powder]] per [[Frozen Corpses|Frozen Corpse]] looted
- 10 [[Glacite Powder]] per [[Cold]] endured
Clicking on the floating text at the Entrance will show a list of these bonuses.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Cold Vulnerability increasing by +0.25 in Glacite Mineshafts|BRRR! It's getting colder in here...|
|Reaching -25 [[Cold]]|BRRR! It's getting really cold in here! But you've got to keep moving...|
|Reaching -50 [[Cold]]|BRRR! It's so cold that you can barely feel your fingers. Moving is getting difficult...|
|Reaching -75 [[Cold]]|BRRR! Your movement slows to a crawl as the cold threatens to take over. Time to get out of here...|
|Reaching -90 [[Cold]]|BRRR! You're freezing! All you can think about is getting out of here to a warm campfire...|
|Reaching -90 [[Cold]]|The following title also appears:FROSTBITE! Find warmth before you freeze to death...|
|Reaching -100 [[Cold]] and dying, in Glacite Mineshafts|You froze to death.; Maybe next time you'll find a way to escape the cold...|
## Tips
- The easiest way to leave a Mineshaft is by using an [[Ascension Rope]].
- Most teleportation and movement abilities are disabled in Glacite Mineshafts, though the [[Grappling Hook]], [[Leaping Sword]], and [[Silk-Edge Sword]] function normally.
## Trivia
- If the player dies while in a Glacite Mineshaft through any means such as [[Cold]] or mobs, they return to the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] and are unable to re-enter.

# Glacite Tunnels
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs
[Glacite Tunnels]
Type: [[Mining]] location
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): Travel Scroll to Dwarven Base Camp ([[Travel Scrolls]])
Level: [[Heart of the Mountain]] level 7
Sub-locations: [[Grandpa Wolf's Cave]] [[Great Glacite Lake]]
Fairy Souls: 2
Music: "Glacite Tunnels ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dwarven Mines]]
Coordinates: 9 121 247
← Previous Location: [[Dwarven Base Camp]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Fossil Muncher]] [[Grandpa Wolf]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Glacite]] [[Umber]] [[Tungsten]] [[Gemstone]] [[Mithril]] [[Titanium]] [[Hard Stone]]
The Glacite Tunnels are a [[Locations|Location]] focused on [[Mining]] that requires [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII]] to be accessed. It has several unique minerals, including [[Glacite]], [[Umber]], and [[Tungsten]].
## Accessing
In order to access the [[Dwarven Base Camp]] and then the Glacite Tunnels, the player must forge a [[Secret Railroad Pass]] after talking to [[Dulin]]. The player have [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VII (7)]] in order to forge a [[Secret Railroad Pass]], forged with 1 [[Gemstone|Flawless Ruby Gemstone]], 2 [[Refined Mithril]], and 8 [[Corleonite]] for 30 seconds.
## Purpose
The Glacite Tunnels has the biggest potential for money-making for miners. In the Glacite Tunnels, the player can mine [[Dwarven Metals]] like [[Umber]], [[Tungsten]] and [[Glacite]]. The Glacite Tunnels also contain [[Gemstone|Gemstones]], exclusive to this area, such as [[Gemstone|Onyx]], [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]] and [[Gemstone|Peridot]]. Inside the Tunnels, you accumulate [[Cold]], which reduces your [[Mining Speed]] by 0.5% per [[Cold]]. Additionally, the player's [[Speed]] is decreased by 25% for every 25 [[Cold]]. Upon reaching 100 [[Cold]], the player freezes to death and is returned to the [[Dwarven Base Camp]].
### Mineshafts
Glacite Mineshafts can be found by mining blocks within the Glacite Tunnels. The base chance of spawning a Glacite Mineshaft is 0.05%, or 1 in 2,000. A pity system was implemented in Version 0.20.1 to provide a more consistent spawn rate for Glacite Mineshafts. Each pity counter begins at 2,000 and decreases by the numbers below for each block type. Once a Glacite Mineshaft has spawned, the counter is reset to 2,000.
|Block Broken|Counter Increment|
|-|-|
|[[Mithril]]|2|
|[[Umber]] [[Tungsten]] [[Glacite]]|4|
|[[Gemstone]] Aquamarine ([[Gemstone]]) Onyx ([[Onyx (disambiguation)]]) Citrine ([[Gemstone]]) Peridot ([[Gemstone]]) [[Titanium]]|8|
After finding a Glacite Mineshaft, a portal will appear, which the player has 30 seconds to enter before it disappears. A sound will be played and the following chat message will appear:
Mineshaft! A mineshaft portal spawned nearby!
A notification will also appear in the middle of the screen with the same wording as the chat message. Once the portal appears, it will emit purple and white particles. Above the particles, the player's name will appear next to Mineshaft Portal. Once you click the portal, a UI will appear with more info:

|[[Grandpa Wolf]]|[[Grandpa Wolf]]|[[Grandpa Wolf's Cave]]; -55.5, 125, 331.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to Frozen Corpse Milestones ([[Frozen Corpses]]).|✕|

|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Reaching -25 [[Cold]]|BRRR! It's getting really cold in here! But you've got to keep moving...|
|Reaching -50 [[Cold]]|BRRR! It's so cold that you can barely feel your fingers. Moving is getting difficult...|
|Reaching -75 [[Cold]]|BRRR! Your movement slows to a crawl as the cold threatens to take over. Time to get out of here...|
|Reaching -90 [[Cold]]|BRRR! You're freezing! All you can think about is getting out of here to a warm campfire...|

## Tips
- It is recommended to enter the Glacite Tunnels after reaching [[Heart of the Mountain|Heart of the Mountain VIII (8)]], as the lowest new gemstones have 9 [[Breaking Power]] and a [[Block Strength]] of 5200, while the newest [[Dwarven Metals]] have 9 [[Breaking Power]] and a block strength of 5,600.
  - As such, the recommended stats and gear are 3750 [[Mining Speed]], [[Armor of Divan]], a [[Silverfish Pet]], and a [[Titanium Drill DR-X555]] or better.
- ᠅ [[Glacite Powder]] from tiers 8-10 can be used to get Warm Hearted and Strong Arm to stay in the Glacite Tunnels and Glacite Mineshafts for longer and mine [[Umber]], [[Tungsten]], and [[Glacite]] quicker, respectively.
  - Additionally, Eager Adventurer, Metal Head, and Rags to Riches can help with mining in the [[Glacite Mineshafts]].
  - Crystalline may be useful if the player needs [[Gemstone Crystals]] to forge [[Gemstone|Onyx]], [[Gemstone|Citrine]], [[Gemstone|Peridot]], [[Gemstone|Aquamarine]], or [[Gemstone|Opal]] [[Gemstone|Perfect Gemstones]].

# Glowing Mushroom Cave
Categories: Locations
[Glowing Mushroom Cave]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to the Glowing Mushroom Cave]]
Level: Farming V (5) ([[Farming]])
Sub-locations: [[Underground Lab]]
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: None
Location: [[Mushroom Desert]]
Coordinates: 206 42 -514
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Beth]] [[Jake]] [[Moby]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): Mooshroom ([[Mushroom Cow]])
[Resources]
Resources: Red Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) Brown Mushroom ([[Mushroom]]) [[Glowing Mushroom]]
The Glowing Mushroom Cave is a location in the [[Mushroom Desert]]. It is the continuation of the [[Overgrown Mushroom Cave]] and it can be entered from [[Coordinates|244, 54, -503]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Glowing Mushroom Cave, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Glowing Mushroom Cave
■ Farm mushrooms.
■ Meet Moby.
After the addition of the [[Moby]], [[Glowing Mushroom|Glowing Mushrooms]] were added. Regular mushroom randomly will start to produce particles, indicating that they have become a Glowing Mushroom. Upon harvesting Glowing Mushrooms players will receive +1 (+2 with [[Moby's Shears]]) Glowing Mushrooms in their inventory. These can then be traded with the NPC [[Moby]].
There is also a hidden room under a coarse dirt block at 175 43 -470 that can be entered after getting note from [[Beth]] in [[The Garden]].
## Location
The Glowing Mushroom Cave is a blueish cave found next to the [[Mushroom Gorge]] and the [[Overgrown Mushroom Cave]]. There are 2 small lakes inside as well as blue crystals hanging from the ceiling. The area has various [[Mushroom]] Trees as well as normal [[Mushroom|Mushrooms]] and it is one of the places where [[Mushroom Cow|Mooshrooms]] can spawn.
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Glowing Mushroom Cave:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|

## Purpose
The Goblin Burrows is home to [[Goblin|Goblins]], [[Knifethrower|Knifethrowers]], and [[Fireslinger|Fireslingers]]. It features a passageway surrounded by gold blocks that opens up into a separate chamber.

# Goblin Holdout
Aliases: Goblin Hideout
Categories: Locations, Article stubs, Crystal Hollows
[Goblin Holdout]
Goblin Holdout.png This image shows a randomly generated Goblin Holdout.
Type: [[Mining]] location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Sub-locations: [[Goblin Queen's Den]]
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[King Yolkar]] [[Xalx]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Goblin]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: Goblin Boots ([[Goblin Armor]]) Goblin Chestplate ([[Goblin Armor]]) [[Goblin Egg]] Goblin Helmet ([[Goblin Armor]]) Goblin Leggings ([[Goblin Armor]])
Resources: *Hard Stone Amber ([[Gemstone]])
The Goblin Holdout is a randomly-generated area in the [[Crystal Hollows]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Goblin Holdout, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Goblin Holdout
■ Home of Goblins.
■ Rich with Amber Gemstones.
## Purpose
[[Goblin|Goblins]] spawn here similarly to the [[Goblin Burrows]] (except that higher level variants can spawn around important [[Crystal Hollows]] [[Crystal Hollows/Special Locations|structures]], those being: " [[King Yolkar]]'s Tower" and the so called "[[Crystal Hollows/Special Locations|Deep Goblin Lair]]").
In the sublocation [[Goblin Queen's Den]], player can obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]].
[[Ores|Nether Quartz Ore]] and [[Netherrack]] spawns very rarely, but if they are mined they drop nothing except some [[Experience|Experience Orbs]] in the case of the former.
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Goblin Holdout:
|Image|NPC|Minimap|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[King Yolkar]]|[[King Yolkar]]|Goblin Holdout; Moves Around|Goblin Holdout; Moves Around|Applies the [[King's Scent]] effect to the player in exchange for 1 [[Goblin Egg]], so they can obtain the Amber Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).|✕|
|[[Xalx]]|[[Xalx]]|Goblin Holdout; Moves Around|Goblin Holdout; Moves Around|Gives the player [[Mysterious Meat]].|✕|

See more stubs.

- A [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Water]] pond can be consistently found at [[Coordinates|358, 167, 534]].

# Goblin Queen's Den
Aliases: Goblin Queen, Goblin Queen Den
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows
[Goblin Queen's Den]
Type: [[Mining]] Location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Goblin Holdout]]
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): Goblins ([[Goblin (disambiguation)]])
The Goblin Queen's Den is a location in the [[Crystal Hollows]], more specifically the [[Goblin Holdout]], where the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]] can be obtained.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Goblin Queen's Den, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Goblin Queen's Den
■ Buried deep underground away from prying eyes.
■ The mythic Goblin Queen lays in wait.
■ She seeks replacements for her now missing King...
## Purpose
After giving 1 [[Goblin Egg]] to [[King Yolkar]] to obtain the [[Potions|King's Scent]] [[Potions|effect]], the player can enter the Goblin Queen's Den to obtain the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]].
## Trivia
- Using a [[Wishing Compass]] while [[Potions|King's Scent]] is active points to the Goblin Queen's Den.
- Prior to 0.20.6, the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]] could be claimed multiple times with one [[Potions|King's Scent]].
## Gallery
- A goblin guard
- The Goblin Queen
- The Amber Crystal The [[Gemstone Crystals|Amber Crystal]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Goblin Queen's Den.|
|Aug 20, 2024|Claiming the Amber Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) now removes King's Scent ([[Potions]]).|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Goblin Queen's Den.|
|Sep 17, 2024|0.20.6|Claiming the Amber Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) now removes King's Scent ([[Potions]]).|

# Gold Mine

[Gold Mine]
Type: [[Mining]] Location
