The Gold Mine is a location that is focused on [[Mining]]. It has various ores and houses [[Rusty]]. [[Mining|Mining I (1)]] is required to access the Gold Mine.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Gold Mine, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Gold Mine
■ Talk to the Lazy Miner.
■ Mine for gold, iron, and coal.
■ Visit the Iron and Gold Forgers.
■ Visit the Blacksmith.
■ Talk to Rusty.
## Purpose
The mine consists of [[Ores|Coal Ore]], [[Ores|Iron Ore]], [[Ores|Gold Ore]] and [[Stone]].
## NPCs
There are 6 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Gold Mine:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|

|[[Gold Forger]]|[[Gold Forger]]|Gold Mine; -27.5, 74, -294.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells gold-related items.|✔|
|[[Iron Forger]]|[[Iron Forger]]|Gold Mine; -1.5, 75, -307.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells iron-related items.|✔|
|[[Lazy Miner]]|[[Lazy Miner]]|Gold Mine; -11.5, 78, -337.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Tasks the player with finding their lost [[Iron Pickaxe]].|✕|
|[[Romero]]|[[Romero]]|Gold Mine; -21.5, 36, -338.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here during the sixth step of the [[Romero and Juliette]] quest, and asks for an [[Enchanted Lava Bucket]].|✕|
|[[Rusty]]|[[Rusty]]|Gold Mine; -19, 78, -325.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various one-time items that the player may have lost.|✔|

## Trivia
- The [[Blacksmith]] in the Gold Mine is the same one as the one in the [[Village]].
- If traveled to without the launcher (with an [[Aspect of the End]] or [[Grappling Hook]]), the inside of the Gold Mine is partly made of [[Sponge]]. Such is the case with many other areas, like the former [[Blazing Fortress]] or [[Crimson Isle]].
- The minecart can be pushed inside the wall, then nudged away in order to get it off of the track.

## Overview
The Grand Library features three main [[NPC|NPCs]], including a [[Castle Guards|Castle Guard]], [[Dalbrek]], who talks about the [[Cult of the Fallen Star]], and [[Tal Ker]], who talks a lot. The left side of the library leads to a room with the [[Puzzler]], who allows the player to gain daily ᠅ [[Mithril Powder]].

# Grandma's House
Categories: Locations
[Grandma's House]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat I (1) ([[Combat]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Music: "Journey in the Sky ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Spider's Den]]
Coordinates: -280 114 -190
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Bramass Beastslayer]] [[Grandma Wolf]] [[Shaggy]]
Grandma's House is a sub-location of the [[Spider's Den]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Grandma's House, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Grandma's House
■ Talk to Bramass.
■ Help out little old Grandma.
■ Find out how to make Shaggy happy.
## Features
Situated on the top floor of Grandma's House, by the bed is [[Grandma Wolf]], an [[NPC]] that rewards the player the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]]. They start the player off with a [[Rarity|COMMON]] [[Grandma Wolf Pet|Pet]], and upgrade it all the way up to [[Rarity|LEGENDARY]] after the player reaches certain [[Bestiary]] milestones.
On the same floor as [[Grandma Wolf]] is [[Shaggy]], an [[NPC]] that gives the player [[Arachne Crystal|Arachne Crystals]] in exchange for a variety of items. Before doing so, the player must have found all 28 [[Relics]] and the additional "rare" relics.
## NPCs
There are 3 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in Grandma's House:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Bramass Beastslayer]]|[[Bramass Beastslayer]]|Grandma's House; -271.5, 113, -196.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to [[Bestiary]].|✕|
|[[Grandma Wolf]]|[[Grandma Wolf]]|Grandma's House; -281.5, 122, -190.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Gives the player the [[Grandma Wolf Pet]].|✕|
|[[Shaggy]]|[[Shaggy]]|Grandma's House; -278.5, 121, -182.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Introduces the player to [[Arachne]].|✔|
## Fairy Souls
There is 1 [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] found in Grandma's House:
|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|SD-10|-279|127|-177|On the roof, near the chimney|✕|
## Trivia
- Whilst the [[Tyashoi Alchemist]] is in the [[Village]], the player can attack the cage in Grandma's House with a [[Scavenged Iron Hammer]] to grant the player a [[Free Spider]].

# Gravel Mines
Aliases: Gravel Mine
Categories: Locations, Confirmations needed
[Gravel Mines]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat I (1) ([[Combat]])
Fairy Souls: 2
Music: "Journey in the Sky ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Spider's Den]]

The Gravel Mines are a sub-location of the [[Spider's Den]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Gravel Mines, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Gravel Mines
■ Mine Gravel
■ Talk to Rick.
## Features
The Gravel Mines features multiple attractions.
### Gravel
The walls of the Gravel Mines are covered in [[Gravel]]. The blocks can be mined to grant [[Gravel]] or [[Flint]], and in some cases both.
There is a room hidden by [[Gravel]] with a [[Relics|Relic]] located in the center [[Coordinates|-206, 63, -301]]. The player must mine blocks to access the room.
### Rick
[[Rick]] is an [[NPC]] located below one of the entrances to the Gravel Mines, standing on a wooden platform. They task the player with finding 2 [[Iron Ingot|Iron Ingots]], and in return, [[Rick]] will give them a [[Promising Shovel]].

# Graveyard
Aliases: Dante Memorial, Dante's Memorial, Dante's memorial, Emerald Altar
Categories: Locations
[Graveyard]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Sub-locations: [[Tavern]]
Fairy Souls: 3
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Hub Island]]
Coordinates: -99 71 -54
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Bartender]] [[Maddox the Slayer]] [[Pat]] [[Romero]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Graveyard Zombie]] [[Zombie Villager]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Carrot]] [[Poisonous Potato]] [[Potato]] [[Rotten Flesh]]
The Graveyard is a [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Hub Island]] found past the [[Combat Settlement]] that features low-level [[Graveyard Zombie|Graveyard Zombies]] and a portal to the [[Spider's Den]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Graveyard, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Graveyard
■ Fight Zombies.
■ Travel to the Spider's Den.
■ Talk to Pat.
■ Investigate the Catacombs.
## Purpose
The Graveyard is home to [[Graveyard Zombie|Graveyard Zombies]], which spawn in during the day, and more frequently at night. Additionally, [[Zombie Villager|Zombie Villagers]] that spawn exclusively and frequently at night.
The back of the Graveyard contains a launch pad to the [[Spider's Den]]. There are also entrances to the [[Crypts]], [[Catacombs Entrance]], and [[Coal Mine]].
### Pat's House
Pat's House
There is a house near the entrance with [[Pat]] inside who sells [[Flint]] and [[Gravel]].
### Emerald Altar
The Emerald Altar
The Emerald Altar is a mysterious altar located in the Graveyard. It consists of 2 Block of Emerald and Lime Stained Glass, making a spire in the center of 8 wide pillars surrounding the spire and thus making the altar.
When the player tries to destroy the emerald block, the following message appears:
The altar doesn't budge!
[[Romero]] is found standing next to it during the 3rd phase of his quest, where he gives you the [[Romero and Juliette Quest Items|Fake Emerald Altar]].
### Dante Memorial
The Dante Memorial
A memorial to [[Dante]] exists at [[Coordinates|-84, 76, -152]]. The stone pillar structure was added into the game during the 0.8.1 Hub revamp, while the [[Dante]] statue and the memorial itself was added after [[Resistance Movement|The Uprising]]. Upon interaction with the stone statue of Dante, the following chat message appears:
In Memoriam
While Dante will be remembered as a cruel tyrant, his righteous ideals live on in people's hearts.
dante best.

# Great Beanstalk
Aliases: Beanstalk
Categories: Locations
[Great Beanstalk]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Dreadfarm]]
Coordinates: 93 72 -146
[Metadata]
ID: BEANSTALK
The Great Beanstalk is a sub-location of the [[Dreadfarm]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
The Great Beanstalk starts as a small sprout, needing to be watered with a [[Plumber's Bucket]]. After being watered, any players in the zone must move into the safe zone, indicated by green particles, to allow the sprout to grow and place more bulbs at the top. This happens multiple times before the Great Beanstalk reaches its full height and spawns an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] at the top.
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in the Great Beanstalk:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Beanstalk|-106.5|249|-149.5|At the top of the Great Beanstalk.|
## Trivia
- If one player goes to the safe leaf, all remaining players on the Great Beanstalk get automatically teleported to it.
## History

|May 4, 2023|Added Great Beanstalk.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Great Beanstalk.|

The Great Glacite Lake is a sub-location in the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. It has lake frozen by [[Glacite]] and surrounded by [[Gemstone|Aquamarine Gemstone]] veins.
## Purpose
The Great Glacite Lake is the site of the [[Dead Man's Hunt]].
Currently, fishing in the Great Glacite Lake does not provide unique [[Sea Creatures]], as hinted by the [[Penguin Pet]]'s ability in the [[Glacite Tunnels]]. However, it is still possible to fish up standard [[Sea Creatures]] to apply the [[Penguin Pet]]'s ability.

# Great Ice Wall
Aliases: Ice Wall
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines
[Great Ice Wall]
Type: [[Combat]] Location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]])

[Resources]

The Great Ice Wall is a location in the [[Dwarven Mines]]. It serves as the barrier between the [[Dwarven Mines]] and [[Glacite Tunnels]].
## Overview
The Great Ice Wall can be accessed through [[Divan's Gateway]] from the [[Cliffside Veins]], the [[Goblin Burrows]] from the [[Far Reserve]], or the [[Aristocrat Passage]] through the [[Royal Mines]].
[[Glacite Walker|Glacite Walkers]] spawn in the Great Ice Wall. The [[Dwarven Base Camp]] can be seen beyond the wall, and can be accessed by giving the [[Station Master]] a [[Secret Railroad Pass]].

# Gunpowder Mines
Categories: Locations, Deep Caverns, Mining, Ores
[Gunpowder Mines]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])

[Resources]

Resources: [[Cobblestone]] [[Coal]] Iron ([[Iron Ingot]]) Gold ([[Gold Ingot]])
[Metadata]

The Gunpowder Mines is the first floor of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Gunpowder Mines, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Gunpowder Mines
■ Talk to the Lift Operator.
■ Explore the caverns.
■ Mine Coal, Iron, and Gold Ore.
■ Watch out for Sneaky Creepers!
## Features
The Gunpowder Mines features a few attractions.
### Lift Operator
In front of the lift shaft is the [[Lift Operator]], who allows players to teleport between layers of the [[Deep Caverns]] with ease. Once the player has entered the Gunpowder Mines and talked to the [[Lift Operator]], they will be able to teleport to the floor from any other level of the island.
### Weak Wall
Located behind [[Walter]] is a weak wall made of cracked and non-cracked stone bricks. The wall can be exploded using either [[Superboom TNT]] or [[InfiniBoom™ TNT]], revealing a small room containing a [[Fairy Souls|Fairy Soul]] in the corner.

|[[Walter]]|[[Walter]]|Gunpowder Mines; 19, 156, -36 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells [[Superboom TNT]] for 2,500 [[Coins]].|✔|

# Half-Eaten Cave
Categories: Locations
[Half-Eaten Cave]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Coordinates: -2 68 8
[Resources]
Resources: [[Half-Eaten Carrot]]
[Metadata]
ID: HALF_EATEN_CAVE
The Half-Eaten Cave is a [[Locations|Location]] within the [[Village Plaza]] of the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Features
The entirety of the Half-Eaten Cave is filled with hay. This cannot be destroyed normally, and instead requires the player to shoot horses with a [[Horsezooka]]. The hay re-grows, and can crush both the player and their rabbit.
If the player's rabbit is squashed, a message will appear, saying:
SPLASH! Your rabbit [Rabbit Name] has been squashed!
Additionally, if the player is squashed, they will lose 60 [[Rift Time]] and be teleported to the entrance of the cave. The following message will appear:
SPLOOSH! You've been squashed by hay!
If the player is squashed while having an active rabbit, it will die regardless of whether it was squashed, with the message:
[Rabbit Name] vanished in terror...
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in the Half-Eaten Cave:
|Name|X|Y|Z|Description|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|Horsing Around|40.5|70|27.5|Towards the end of the cave.|

## Tasks

NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Howling Cave

[[Old Shaman Nyko]] is the sole [[NPC]] located in the Howling Cave. They sell two items: [[True Essence]], which can be used to brew [[True Resistance Potion|True Resistance Potions]], and a [[Enchantments|True Protection]] [[Enchanted Book]], which can be applied to [[Chestplates]] to grant a small increase to [[True Defense]].

# Hub Island
Aliases: Hub, Hub World, Overworld, SkyBlock Hub, The Hub
Categories: Locations
[Hub Island]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Travel Scroll ([[Travel Scrolls]]): [[Travel Scroll to Hub Castle]]; [[Travel Scroll to Dark Auction]]; [[Travel Scroll to Hub Crypts]]; [[Travel Scroll to Museum]]; [[Travel Scroll to the Wizard Tower]]
Sub-locations: [[Abiphones & Co.]] [[Archery Range]] [[Artist's Abode]] [[Auction House]] [[Bank]] [[Bazaar Alley]] Blacksmith ([[Blacksmith (Zone)]]) [[Builder's House]] [[Canvas Room]] [[Carnival]] [[Coal Mine]] [[Colosseum]] [[Combat Settlement]] [[Community Center]] [[Crypts]] [[Dark Auction]] [[Election Room]] [[Farm]] [[Farmhouse]] [[Fashion Shop]] [[Fisherman's Hut]] [[Fishing Outpost]] [[Flower House]] [[Foraging Camp]] [[Forest]] [[Graveyard]] [[Library]] [[Mining District]] [[Mountain]] [[Museum]] [[Rabbit House]] [[Regalia Room]] [[Ruins]] [[Security Hall]] [[Sewer]] [[Shen's Auction]] [[Tavern]] [[Trade Center]] [[Thaumaturgist]] [[Unincorporated]] [[Village]] [[Wilderness]] [[Wizard Tower]]
Fairy Souls: 80
Music: "Hub ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
[Metadata]
ID: HUB
The Hub Island is the first island that the player can travel to upon leaving their [[Private Island]]. It is a centralised island that connects to a multitude of other islands: the [[Village]] can be found at the center, which branches off into specialised zones, each revolving around different [[Skills]].
## Locations
The Hub Island consists of many sub-locations, each with their own purpose.
|Location|Description|NPCs Found|
|-|-|-|
|[[Village]]|The spawn point and center area of the Hub Island. Splits off into other areas focused on specific [[Skills]].|[[Agnes]], [[Alcina]], [[Alixer]], [[Amelia]], [[Baker]], [[Bingo (NPC)]], [[Christopher]], [[Chuckleton]], [[Fear Mongerer]], [[Fisherwoman Enid]], [[Guy]], [[Hoppity]], [[Hub Selector]], [[Hypixel Discord]], [[Jerry]], [[Lukas the Aquarist]], [[Piggy]], [[Romero]], [[Salesman]], [[Torrhus Canyon (NPC)]]|

|^ [[Bank]]|Features the [[Banker]], who allows the player to store their [[Coins]].|[[Banker]]|
|^ [[Bazaar Alley]]|Allows the player to access the [[Bazaar]] to purchase commodity items.|[[Bazaar (NPC)]], [[Bazaar Agent]]|
|^ [[Builder's House]]|Features merchants who sell blocks for building.|[[Builder]], [[Mad Redstone Engineer]], [[Wool Weaver]]|

# Hub Well
Categories: Locations, Village
[Hub Well]
Type: Shop
Coordinates: 16.5 72 -3.5
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Piggy]] [[Lukas the Aquarist]] [[Agnes]] [[Alcina]]
The Hub Well is a structure found n the [[Village]] between spawn and the [[Museum]]. During zodiac events, it is replaced with a different structure themed around that event, including the [[Year of the Pig]], [[Year of the Seal]], and [[Year of the Witch]].
## Cycle
The following merchant NPCs each appear for one [[Time Systems|SkyBlock year]] on a 12-year cycle.
|Rem. (The remainder when dividing the SkyBlock year by 12.)|Event|NPCs|Countdown|
|-|-|-|-|
|0|||: Countdown:|
|1|||: Countdown:|
|2|||: Countdown:|
|3|||: Countdown:|
|4|||: Countdown:|
|5|||: Countdown:|
|6|[[Year of the Seal]]|Lukas ([[Lukas the Aquarist]])|: Countdown:|
|7|||: Countdown:|
|8|[[Year of the Witch]]|[[Alcina]], [[Agnes]]|: Countdown:|
|9|||: Countdown:|
|10|||: Countdown:|
|11|[[Year of the Pig]]|[[Piggy]]|: Countdown:|
## Gallery
- The Hub Well
- Hub Well during the Year of the Pig Hub Well during the [[Year of the Pig]]
### Before 0.24.2
- The original Hub Well
## Trivia
- The 12 year rotation is based on the Chinese zodiac.
## History
[[SkyBlock Prototype]]:
|Jun 11, 2019|0.1|Added Hub Well.|
|Apr 16, 2021|Temporarily replaced the well with the [[Blood God Shrine]].|
|May 8, 2021|Restored the original well.|
|Feb 11, 2025|0.21.3|Added the [[Year of the Seal]] (starting year 402) and [[Year of the Pig]] (starting year 407), which each change the Hub Well during one of every 12 SkyBlock years.|
|Feb 24, 2026|0.24.2|Moved and updated the look of the Hub Well, due to the redesign of the [[Hub Island]].|

# Infested House
Categories: Locations
[Infested House]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

[Inhabitants]

[Metadata]
ID: INFESTED_HOUSE
The Infested House is a sub-location of the [[West Village]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Pet Care|Pet House]].
## Features
Surrounding the Infested House are [[Silverfish (disambiguation)|Silverfish]], Flies and Spiders. These can be vacuumed with a [[Turbomax Vacuum Cleaner]] and subsequently deposited in the Vermin Bin on the ground floor of the Infested House. Having deposited enough vermin in the bin allows the player to purchase items from [[Kat (Rift)|Kat]], at a cost.
## NPCs

|[[Disinfestor]]|[[Disinfestor]]|Infested House; -32.5, 77, -88.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about how they usually work in a nuclear power plant.|✕|
|[[Disinfestor]]|[[Disinfestor]]|Infested House; -37.5, 77, -93.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about how they feel like they're supposed to open up a shop menu.|✕|
|[[Kat (Rift)]]|[[Kat (Rift)]]|Infested House; -34.5, 70, -90.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells various items in exchange for vacuuming up vermin.|✔|

# Ironman's Guild
Aliases: Miner's Guild
Categories: Locations, Dwarven Mines
[Ironman's Guild]

Music: "Dwarven Mines ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

[Inhabitants]

The Ironman's Guild is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Dwarven Mines]], located above [[The Lift]]. It serves as an introduction to the [[Ironman]] gamemode.
## Purpose
[[Ian]] introduces the player to [[Ironman]] and lets the player create an [[Ironman]] profile. [[Ivan]] informs the player of the ability to 'de-iron' a profile, where an Ironman profile can be converted into a regular one. [[Ivy]] talks more about Ironman.

|[[Ian]]|[[Ian]]|Ironman's Guild; -67.5, 221, -121.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Ironman]] gamemode.|✕|
|[[Ivan]]|[[Ivan]]|Ironman's Guild; -62.5, 221, -131.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about how [[Ironman]] profiles can be transferred back into regular ones.|✕|
|[[Ivy]]|[[Ivy]]|Ironman's Guild; -72.5, 221, -135.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Talks about the [[Ironman]] gamemode.|✕|

Jake's House is a small wooden house located on the highest mountain. Inside, there's a small cyan bed as well as a small table with a Cauldron. In the middle of the house there's [[Jake]].

# Jerry Pond
Categories: Locations, Events, Season of Jerry, Fishing
[Jerry Pond]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Jerry's Workshop]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Frozen Alex]] [[Gregory]]
[Resources]
Resources: [[Ice]]
[Metadata]
ID: ICE_POND
The Jerry Pond is a [[Locations|Location]] on [[Jerry's Workshop]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Jerry Pond, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Jerry Pond
■ Break through the ice.
■ Try out ice fishing.
■ Talk to Terry in his shack!
## Features
The Jerry Pond's surface naturally turns into [[Ice]], which can be broken. Breaking the ice reveals the water beneath, in which the player can fish up a variety of [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]]. Once the ice on the surface is broken, it will regenerate after a while and must be broken again to reach the water.
When a [[Reindrake]] spawns, [[Gregory]] will spawn in, allowing the player to purchase a [[Decent Bow]] and [[Flint Arrow|Flint Arrows]].
### Frozen Alex
[[Frozen Alex]] is an [[NPC]] in an igloo next to another frozen lake. They offer the player an [[Essence Shops|Ice Essence Shop]], with 4 unique perks, two of which are related to the [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]]. Additionally, [[Frozen Alex]] sells an [[Enchanted Book]] ([[Experience (Enchantment)|Experience V]]) for 15,000x [[Ice Essence]].
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Jerry Pond:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|

[Metadata]
ID: WINTER_ISLAND
Jerry's Workshop, also known as the Winter Island, is an island that is open during the whole season of Late Winter (~11 hours long) every 5 days and 4 hours, or during the whole month of December. However, during the bonus events the Winter Island will only be opened 3 hours, from 1 hour before the event to 1 hour after. The island can be accessed through the [[SkyBlock Menu]], your own [[Jerry]] or the Event Stand in the [[Village]]. Jerry's is the only location where [[Sea Creatures|Winter Sea Creatures]] can be fished up.
[[Frosty]] is located near the entrance. He will reward the player with [[Frosty the Snow Cannon]], which is used in the [[Season of Jerry]] event.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Jerry's Workshop, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Jerry's Workshop
■ Talk to Frosty.
■ Defend the village from enemies.
■ Ice fishing.
■ Exchange Gifts!
## Sublocations
|Sub Location|Description|
|-|-|
|[[Einary's Emporium]]|Purchase winter-themed rewards in exchange for North Stars and other items.|
|[[Gary's Shack]]|Talk to [[Gary]].|
|[[Glacial Cave]]|Collect [[Ice]] and find treasure buried within the ice.|
|[[Hot Springs]]|Fish up Winter Sea Creatures.|
|[[Jerry Pond]]|Collect ice and fish up Winter Sea Creatures.|
|[[Mount Jerry]]|During the Season of Jerry, Mount Jerry will erupt and release lava, Liquid Hot Magmas ([[Defend Jerry's Workshop]]), and Magma Cubes ([[Magma Cube]]). Players must defend the piles of gifts in Jerry's Workshop.|
|[[Reflective Pond]]|Collect ice and fish up Winter Sea Creatures. Talk to [[Frozen Alex]]|
|[[Sherry's Showroom]]|Purchase items from [[Sherry]].|
|[[Sunken Jerry Pond]]|Collect ice and fish up Winter Sea Creatures.|
|[[Terry's Shack]]|Buy special [[Reforge Stones]] for [[Fishing Rods]] from [[Terry]].|
## Quests
### Defend Jerry's Workshop
Mobs will begin to spawn and attack five piles of Gifts in the village on the 23rd Late Winter. Players must defeat the waves of mobs that spawn to protect the [[Gifts]]. These mobs cannot be damaged by regular attacks, and instead must be shot at with snowballs. Once all the [[Magma Cube|Magma Cubes]] have been killed, the event will end and the player may collect [[Gifts]] from any remaining piles in the village. This event has 5 waves lasting a total of 1 hour.
### Chicken Race
[[Gulliver]] tasks the player with controlling a chicken by flying it through hoops in the sky. The race will end if the timer runs out, player touches the ground or leaves the chicken (e.g. dismounting the chicken by pressing Shift, using an [[Aspect of the End]], etc.) Passing through Cluckpoints will grant the player rewards for the first time they are passed and increase the timer.
The player can obtain a [[Red Gift]], [[Gift Compass]], [[Frozen Chicken]], and the [[Jingle Bells]] from the Chicken Race. The [[Frozen Chicken]] can be further upgraded into the [[Fried Frozen Chicken]].
### White Gifts
Every time the [[Jerry's Workshop|Winter Island]] opens, 20 [[White Gift|White Gifts]] are scattered across Jerry's Workshop, which the player is tasked with finding. Between [[Time Systems|SkyBlock years]], the gifts change locations, but can generally be found in the same areas: behind lampposts, snow and on top of rooftops. If the player owns a [[Gift Compass]], they can use it to point in the direction of the nearest undiscovered [[White Gift]].
After collecting all 20 [[White Gift|White Gifts]], the player can talk to [[St. Jerry]] to receive a [[Green Gift]].

# Jungle Island
Categories: Locations
[Jungle Island]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])

The Jungle Island is a sub-location of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Jungle Island, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Jungle Island
■ Chop down Jungle logs.
## Features
The Jungle Island features a few attractions.
### Juliette
[[Juliette]] is an [[NPC]] located on the floating island. She is part of the [[Romero and Juliette]] Quest, and asks the player to get her back with [[Romero]] by giving him certain items. [[Romero]] will give the player an item back, which must be given to [[Juliette]] to progress in the quest.
### Molbert
[[Molbert]] will task you with collecting [[Jungle Log|Jungle Wood]], [[Carrot|Carrots]], and [[String]] to build traps to capture moles that are ruining the beauty of the park. However, he ends up getting trapped in his own traps and reveals that he is indeed a mole, and gives you the [[Mole Hat]] as compensation for your troubles.
### Spirit Cave
Near the middle of the Jungle Island lies a breakable wall that can be blown up with an [[Explosive Bow]] or [[Superboom TNT]]. Here, you will find the [[Spirit Cave]], where [[Pack Spirit|Pack Spirits]], [[Howling Spirit|Howling Spirits]], and [[Soul of the Alpha|Soul of the Alphas]] will spawn. This is one of three caves designed for [[Wolf Slayer]].

# Jungle Temple
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows
[Jungle Temple]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Jungle]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Kalhuiki Tribe Member]] [[Sludge]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Sludge Juice]]
Resources: [[Gemstone]] [[Hard Stone]] [[Sludge Juice]] [[Jungle Heart]] [[Pickonimbus 2000]]
The Jungle Temple is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Jungle]] of the [[Crystal Hollows]] where the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] can be obtained.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Jungle Temple, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Jungle Temple
■ A vast and sprawling underground civilization.
■ Temples and puzzles hide their treasures.
■ ...and deep secrets.
## Mechanics
The Jungle Temple main building houses the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]], which is guarded by a series of 8 simple parkours with traps. 2 [[Crystal Hollows|Loot Chests]] also spawn at the end of the parkour; these chests have higher chances to contain a [[Jungle Heart]]. Using a [[Wishing Compass]] in the [[Jungle]] with a [[Jungle Key]] in the inventory points towards the Jungle Temple.
A [[Jungle Key]] is required to enter the parkour and claim both [[Crystal Hollows|Loot Chests]] and the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] at the end of the parkour.
The door to the Jungle Temple will open when players interact with either [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]] at the entrance using the [[Jungle Key]].
If the player falls into or touch any body of [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] during the run, the player will be teleported back to the entrance.
Claiming the [[Gemstone Crystals|Amethyst Crystal]] prevents the player from claiming it again as well as the surrounding [[Crystal Hollows|Loot Chests]], unless another [[Jungle Key]] is given to the [[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]].
Within the Jungle Temple area, the player's jump boost (from the [[Rabbit Potion|Rabbit]] and [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost]] effects) is removed, and any bonus [[Speed]] is removed, only leaving base [[Speed]].
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Jungle Temple:
|Image|NPC|Minimap|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]]|[[Kalhuiki Door Guardian]]|Jungle Temple; Moves Around|Jungle Temple; Moves Around|Allows the player to access the Jungle Temple parkour by presenting a [[Jungle Key]], so they can obtain the Amethyst Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).|✕|
## Trivia
- The [[Black Cat Pet]] used to be able to bypass the [[Speed]] cap of the Temple.
  - This has since been patched.
- It is possible to enter the Temple without using a [[Jungle Key]], by mining at a precise location near the entrance stairs. However, if the parkour is completed without the usage of a temple key, the player cannot open any loot chests near the crystal and will be presented with this message when trying to obtain the crystal: "You haven't earned this Crystal! Come back when you have >:-)".
- Sometimes, the [[Jump Boost Potion|Jump Boost]] potion effects are not properly removed upon entering the Jungle Temple area. This can be fixed by leaving and re-entering the area or disabling the Rabbit effect in the cookie buff menu.

# Kelpwoven Tunnels
Categories: Locations

The Kelpwoven Tunnels are a sub-location of the [[Moonglade Marsh]].
## Features
The Kelpwoven Tunnels consist of several tunnels filled with coral, where [[Spike|Spikes]] can be hunted. The [[Bubbleboost Column]] can be found here and leads to the [[Wyrmgrove Tomb]] and [[Tangleburg]].

# Khazad-dûm
Aliases: Khazad Dum, Khazad dum, Khazad-dum
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows
[Khazad-dûm]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Magma Fields]]
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Bal]] [[Bald Blaze]] [[Yog]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bal Pet]] [[Blaze Rod]] Flawed Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Flawed Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Yoggie]]
Khazad-dûm is a location in the [[Crystal Hollows]] within the [[Magma Fields]] where the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]] can be obtained.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Khazad-dûm, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Khazad-dûm
■ Deep below the Misty Mountains lies the shadow and flame guarding ancient treasure...
## Purpose
Khazad-dûm is home to [[Bal]], a [[Bosses|boss]] which spawns periodically in the center. After the boss sinks into the ground, the [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]] will spawn close-by and become claimable.
## Trivia
- When using the [[Wishing Compass]] in the [[Magma Fields]], it points towards the Khazad-dûm.
- Khazad-dûm is a reference to a place of the same name in J. R. R. Tolkien's The Lord of the Rings.
## Gallery
- The Topaz Crystal The [[Gemstone Crystals|Topaz Crystal]]

[SkyBlock Prototype]

# Kuudra's Hollow
Categories: Locations, Kuudra, Crimson Isle
[Kuudra's Hollow]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Combat XXII (22) ([[Combat]])
Music: "Lava Kraken ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Elle]] [[Weird Sailor]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Kuudra]] [[Blazing Golem]] [[Blight]] [[Chaosmite]] [[Dropship]] [[Explosive Imp]] [[Inferno Magma Cube]] [[Kuudra Berserker]] [[Kuudra Follower]] [[Kuudra Knocker]] [[Kuudra Landmine]] [[Kuudra Slasher]] [[Magma Bacteria]] [[Magma Follower]] [[Omegagma]] [[Wandering Blaze]] [[Wither Sentry]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Bone Dye]]
Kuudra's Hollow is a [[Locations|Location]] accessed from the [[Crimson Isle]]. It serves as the home of [[Kuudra]] and its boss fight.
## Purpose
Kuudra's Hollow is where [[Kuudra]] spawns, and the fight takes place. It is a massive space, with a bridge leading to a large platform, surrounded by [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]].
At the start of the fight, the bridge will collapse, leaving players only to the center platform. In the [[Kuudra|Fourth Phase]] in Burning Tier and higher, players will be able to enter Kuudra's stomach, leading to a separate area. In the [[Kuudra|Infernal Tier]] of the fight, the center of the arena will collapse, revealing a lower section of the arena.
At the end of the fight, [[Kuudra|Loot Chests]] will spawn, one free, and one paid for by [[Kuudra Keys]], if Kuudra was successfully defeated. Additionally, if the player has donated 1,000,000 [[Coins]] to the [[Kuudra Believer]] in the [[Plhlegblast Pool]] within the last 30 minutes, the [[Weird Sailor]] will appear for them on the edge of the arena. He hints at a list of conditions needed to fish up the [[Wet Napkin]].
## NPCs
There are 2 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in Kuudra's Hollow:
|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Elle]]|[[Elle]]|Kuudra's Hollow; -101.5, 79.12, -175.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here at the start of the [[Kuudra]] fight.|✕|
|[[Weird Sailor]]|[[Weird Sailor]]|Kuudra's Hollow; -131, 93, -177 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to fish up the [[Wet Napkin]] under certain conditions.|✕|

# Lagoon Cave
Categories: Locations
[Lagoon Cave]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Black Lagoon]]
Coordinates: -56 77 35
[Metadata]
ID: LAGOON_PARKOUR
The Lagoon Cave is a sub-location of the [[Black Lagoon]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Purpose
Throughout the Lagoon Cave is a parkour course which leads all the way from the area's entrance in the [[Black Lagoon]] until the end of the cave. There are platforms made of Polished Granite on which the player must traverse the cave. If the player comes in contact with the dirt or water below the platforms they will be teleported to the beginning and must start again.
At the end of the parkour is an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] and a Teleport Pad leading Out! which when stepped on will teleport the player back to the entrance of the Lagoon Cave.
## Game Messages
|Condition|Dialogue|
|-|-|
|Touching Water|The water is angry! Its anger somehow teleported you back!|
|Touching Dirt|The dirt is angry! Its anger somehow teleported you back!|

|-|-|-|-|-|

# Lagoon Hut
Categories: Locations
[Lagoon Hut]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Enigma Souls: 1

[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Kay]] [[Reed]] [[Roy]]
[Metadata]
ID: LAGOON_HUT
The Lagoon Hut is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It is based on the [[Fisherman's Hut]].
## Features
The Lagoon Hut features a few attractions. The player must cross the [[Black Lagoon]] with [[Lil' Pad|Lil' Pads]] in order to access the location.
### Reed
Located at the back of the Lagoon Hut is [[Reed]], an [[NPC]] who tasks the player with obtaining different items. There are 5 steps, each one requiring a different amount and type of item. After completing all five, [[Reed]] will offer the player [[Rift Boat]] for 25,000 [[Motes]], which is needed in order to access the [[Stillgore Château]].
### The Gill-Man
One of [[Porhtal]]'s eyes, The Gill-Man, can be found in the basement of the Lagoon Hut. The eye's condition is ROGUE, and can be defeated to gain a permanent +20 [[Rift Time]] and the ability to instantly teleport to it from [[Porhtal]] or any of their other eyes. Teleporting to The Gill-Man costs 5s [[Rift Time]] if the player has entered the [[Black Lagoon]] and can only be done if the player has enough time remaining.
## NPCs

|[[Reed]]|[[Reed]]|Lagoon Hut; -212, 72, 60.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Rift Boat]].|✔|
|[[Roy]]|[[Roy]]|Lagoon Hut; -204.5, 75, 49 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells an Enigma Soul ([[Enigma Souls]]) for 4,000 [[Motes]] and 8 Deadgehog Spines ([[Deadgehog Spine]]).|✕|

# Lapis Quarry
Categories: Locations, Mining, Deep Caverns, Ores
[Lapis Quarry]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Location: [[Deep Caverns]]
← Previous Location: [[Gunpowder Mines]]

ID: DEEP_CAVERNS_ROOM_2
The Lapis Quarry is the second floor of the [[Deep Caverns]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Lapis Quarry, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Lapis Quarry
■ The Lapis Quarry can now be traveled to via the Lift Operator.
■ Mine Lapis Lazuli Ore.
■ Talk to the Lapis Miner.
■ Watch out for Lapis Zombies!
## Features
The Lapis Quarry features a few attractions.
### Lift Operator
In front of the lift shaft is the [[Lift Operator]], who allows players to teleport between layers of the [[Deep Caverns]] with ease. Once the player has entered the Lapis Quarry and talked to the [[Lift Operator]], they will be able to teleport to the floor from any other level of the island.
### Lapis Miner
At the back of the small rounded cave at the far end of the Lapis Quarry, the [[Lapis Miner]] can be found. They are an [[NPC]] who asks the player to bring them a [[Lapis Pickaxe]], which requires reaching [[Lapis Lazuli|Lapis Lazuli III (3)]] to craft. Upon giving them one, they award the player with a [[Compactor]] and [[SkyBlock Levels|SkyBlock XP]].
## NPCs

|[[Lift Operator]]|[[Lift Operator]]|Lapis Quarry; 45.5, 121, 15.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Allows the player to teleport to other parts of the [[Deep Caverns]], or the [[Dwarven Mines]].|✕|

# Leeches Lair
Categories: Locations
[Leeches Lair]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])

Location: [[Black Lagoon]]

ID: LEECHES_LAIR
The Leeches Lair is a sub-location of the [[Black Lagoon]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Features
The Leeches Lair features a few attractions.
### Leech Supreme
The [[Leech Supreme]] is a boss found in the Leeches Lair. They spawn a few seconds after they were last killed. The [[Leech Supreme]] will cast several different attacks, one at a time, in order to kill the player. Once the player successfully kills the Leech, they will receive [[Leech Supreme Fragment|Leech Supreme Fragments]] and [[Motes]].
### Water
Surrounding the platform in the center of the Leeches Lair is a pool of water. Entering the water removes 1 [[Rift Time]] every second. After a few seconds, the player will be flung out and onto the platform, but if this is unsuccessful, then they will be tried to be flung out again, until they land on the platform. Additionally, there are ladders that the player can climb up to escape from the water.
## NPCs

# Living Cave
Aliases: Intangibility
Categories: Locations
[Living Cave]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])

Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"

NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Rock]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Autonull]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Nullified Metal]]
Resources: [[Living Metal]] [[Living Metal Heart]]
[Metadata]
ID: LIVING_CAVE
The Living Cave is a sub-location of the [[Rift Dimension]]. It can be entered from the [[Village Plaza]], through the [[Déjà Vu Alley]].
The player can see into the Living Cave through the [[Dreadfarm]], but the entrance is blocked off on both ends with Barriers, requiring 'intangibility' to enter - or walking around, telegraphing that the Living Cave is inaccessible from the [[Dreadfarm]].
## Tasks
Viewing the [[Rift Guide]] for the Living Cave will display the following tasks:
Things to do:
⇨ Craft Self-Recursive Pickaxe
⇨ Craft the Crux Heirloom
⇨ Enigma: Behind a Living Wall
⇨ Enigma: Flowery Message
⇨ Montezuma's Eight
Enigma Souls: 2/2
## Features
The Living Cave features many attractions. The player must have secured the [[SkyBlock Citizen Timecharm]] and have a minimum of 5:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining in order to access the location.
### Living Metal Snakes
Living Metal Snakes move around the Living Cave. They appear as chains of Blue Stained Glass with a Block of Lapis Lazuli at the end, indicating its head. Snakes can be stopped with by pointing a [[Frozen Water Pungi]] at them, and can be mined from their tail blocks. They drop [[Living Metal Heart|Living Metal Hearts]] once their heads are broken.
### Living Stillness
The player can access the [[Living Stillness]] by going through the hole in the Living Cave. The player will receive Frostbite and begin losing [[Rift Time]] if their Armor does not have Living Metal applied to it, obtained from [[Living Metal Capspawn]], [[Living Metal Chestspawn]], [[Living Metal Pantspawn]], and [[Living Metal Bootspawn]].
### Enigma Souls
The Living Cave features 2 [[Enigma Souls]].
Enigma Souls:
38, 63, -197 ([[Coordinates]]): 3, 68, -203 ([[Coordinates]])
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|May 4, 2023|Added Living Cave.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jun 20, 2023|0.19|Added Living Cave.|

# Living Stillness

ID: LIVING_CAVE_2
The Living Stillness is a [[Locations|Location]] in the [[Rift Dimension]]. It can be entered through a hole in the floor of the [[Living Cave]].
## Features
### Frostbite
The Living Stillness will apply Frostbite to players, which takes 60s [[Rift Time]] from them per second. This can be prevented by wearing armor that has been 100% Living Metal applied to it, through [[Living Metal Capspawn]], [[Living Metal Chestspawn]], [[Living Metal Pantspawn]], and [[Living Metal Bootspawn]].
### Living Metal
[[Living Metal]] mined in the Living Stillness is doubled.
### Living Metal Snakes
Living Metal Snakes move slower in the Living Stillness than in the [[Living Cave]].

Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Village Plaza]]
Coordinates: -33 66 -24
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Lonely Ävaeìkx]]
[Metadata]
ID: LONELY_TERRACE
The Lonely Terrace is a sub-location of the [[Village Plaza]] in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Features
[[Lonely Ävaeìkx]] is an [[NPC]] located in the Lonely Terrace. They ask the player to sit down in a chair for 3 minutes in exchange for an Enigma Soul. However, [[Lonely Ävaeìkx]] moves from table to table, and if they sit at the same table as the player, they must move to a different one in order to please [[Lonely Ävaeìkx|Ävaeìkx]]. Once the 3 minutes are up, an [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] will be revealed in the center of the Lonely Terrace.
By the entrance to the Lonely Terrace is a hologram, detailing the player's highest time spent sitting with [[Lonely Ävaeìkx]], their ranking, and the total time spent by the [[SkyBlock]] community as a whole sitting in the Lonely Terrace.
LONELY TERRACE
You sat with Ävaeìkx: #m#s
Ranking you [#pos]
SkyBlock players have spent a combined
[#time] sitting here
## Enigma Souls
There is 1 [[Enigma Souls|Enigma Soul]] found in the Lonely Terrace:

|-|-|-|-|-|
|Lonely Ăvaeìkx|-33.5|66|-24.5|Obtainable after sitting with [[Lonely Ävaeìkx]] for at least 3 minutes.|

# Lost Precursor City
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows
[Lost Precursor City]
Type: [[Mining]] Location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Precursor Remnants]]
Precise Location: Varies between lobbies
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Professor Robot]]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Automaton]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Control Switch]] [[Electron Transmitter]] [[FTX 3070]] [[Robotron Reflector]] Rough Sapphire Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Superlite Motor]] [[Synthetic Heart]]
The Lost Precursor City is a location in the [[Crystal Hollows]], more specifically the [[Precursor Remnants]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Lost Precursor City, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Lost Precursor City
■ Ancient machines protecting ancient cities that hide ancient secrets.
## Purpose
The [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]] can be obtained. 6 [[Professor Robot|Automaton Parts]] are required to claim the crystal, each randomly dropped by killing [[Automaton]] or obtained by opening chests in the [[Precursor Remnants]]. Delivering all six parts or a [[Precursor Apparatus]] to [[Professor Robot]] will unlock the door next to him, allowing access to the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]].
## NPCs
There is 1 [[NPC]] found in the Lost Precursor City:
|Image|NPC|Minimap|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Professor Robot]]|[[Professor Robot]]|Lost Precursor City; Moves Around|Lost Precursor City; Moves Around|Allows the player to access the Sapphire Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]) after giving them robot parts.|✕|
## Trivia
- Right-clicking on the blocks in the door to the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]] will cause them to disappear, although the Crystal will not be claimable unless you give all parts to [[Professor Robot]]. The only use it has is to kill the Automatons spawned in the Sapphire Crystal claiming location.
- The door to the [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]] doesn't reappear, even if the crystal is claimed and placed at the [[Crystal Nucleus]].
- There is a room with [[Melon|Melons]] that can be broken, however they only give [[Farming|Farming XP]], no drops or collection.
## Gallery
- The Sapphire Crystal The [[Gemstone Crystals|Sapphire Crystal]]
## History
[[Alpha Hypixel Network]]:
|Jun 30, 2021|AHN 0.12 Round 1|Added Lost Precursor City.|
[SkyBlock Prototype]
|Jul 14, 2021|0.12|Added Lost Precursor City.|
|Jul 20, 2021|[[Professor Robot]] will now tells the player which precursor parts are missing.|
|Dec 12, 2023|0.19.9|Fixed being unable to use abilities in the Lost Precursor City.|

Aliases: Atoll, Exploding Lily Pad, Exploding Lily Pads, Lotus Atoll (disambiguation)
Categories: Locations, Confirmations needed

← Previous Location: [[Backwater Bayou]]
Next Location →: None

Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Atoll Croaker]] [[Drowned Captain]] [[Flipflopper]] [[Frog Prince]] [[GorF]] [[Lotum]] [[Lotus Guardian]] [[Lotusfish]] [[Puddle Jumper]] [[Seashine]] [[Tewtil]]
[Resources]

The Lotus Atoll is a [[Fishing]] [[Locations|Location]] and the second island that can be accessed from [[Ship|Ship Navigator]]. It is unlocked by reaching [[Fishing|Fishing X (10)]] and speaking to [[Researcher Gilbert]] on the [[Backwater Bayou]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Lotus Atoll, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Lotus Atoll
■ Learn about Trophy Frogs from Researcher Ribery.
■ Catch the 12 species of Trophy Frog.
■ Trade Frogs with Researcher Ribery in exchange for Lotus.
■ Progress through the Lotus Collection!
## Features
The Lotus Atoll is a [[Fishing]]-oriented location, with a variety of unique features resolving around progress in the skill.
### Chapters
The Lotus Atoll contains [[Chapters]], which consist of 47 tasks for the player to complete. Completing all these tasks rewards the [[Ship|Pearl of the Lotus]], which grants a permanent +2.5 [[Trophy Chance]]. These chapters can be accessed by speaking to [[Researcher Betty]].
Begin your journey as Batrachologist.:

|Catch a BRONZE Trophy Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])|[[Trophy Frogs]] can be exchanged with [[Researcher Ribery]] in return for [[Lotus]].|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|
|Catch an [[Atoll Croaker]]|This is a common Lotus Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]) found on the Lotus Atoll.|+250 Fishing XP ([[Fishing]])|

### Sea Creatures
The Lotus Atoll contains a variety of unique [[Sea Creatures]] to be caught.
|Sea Creature|Mob Type|Fishing Skill|Base Weight|Base Chance|Categories|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|

|[[GorF]] (RARE ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]], [[Arcane]]|10|1500|15%|(same as above)|
|[[Drowned Captain]] (EPIC ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], Undead ([[Undead (Mob Type)]])|10|500|5%|(same as above)|
|[[Puddle Jumper]] (LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]]))|[[Aquatic]], [[Animal]], [[Elusive]]|10|100|1%|(same as above)|

### Treasure
Lotus Atoll has a unique [[Treasure|Treasure Fishing]] loot table.
### Critters
Aside from [[Sea Creatures]], there are various [[Critters]] that can be found on the Lotus Atoll, all of which are tied to [[Hunting]] and cannot be damaged regularly:
- [[Lotusfish]], which can be found swimming in the water and caught with a [[Fishing Nets|Fishing Net]] to obtain the [[Lotusfish Shard]].
- [[Lotum|Lotums]], which can be found around the water. When punched 4 times (or 3 times with [[Attributes|Frog Legs]]), they grant the [[Lotum Shard]].
- [[Tewtil|Tewtils]], which can be found in the water and primarily in the [[Tewtil Tunnel]]. When fed three [[Trophy Frogs]], they explode and grant the [[Tewtil Shard]].
- [[Flipflopper|Flipfloppers]], which have a 50% chance to spawn from an [[Lotus Atoll|Exploding Lily Pad]]. They can be damaged with a [[Medium Fishing Net]] or better.
- [[Seashine|Seashines]], which have a 25% chance to spawn from an [[Lotus Atoll|Exploding Lily Pad]]. They can also be damaged with a [[Medium Fishing Net]] or better.
### Lotus Collection
[[Lotus]] is a [[Collections|Collection]] that is obtained exclusively on the Lotus Atoll. It features items that make it easier to catch [[Trophy Frogs]].
|LVL|Required|Reward|XP ([[SkyBlock Levels]])|
|-|-|-|-|
|I|25|[[Froggles]] Recipe|+4|
|II|100|[[Red Sweater]] Recipe|+4|

|IV|1,000|[[Silver Froggles]] Recipe|+4|

|X|100,000|[[Diamond Lotus]] Recipe|+4|
|XI|250,000|[[Diamond Froggles]] Recipe|+4|
### Trophy Frogs
[[Trophy Frogs]] are a type of item that can be caught exclusively on the Lotus Atoll. Like [[Trophy Fish]], each type of Trophy Frog comes in four tiers: BRONZE, SILVER, GOLD, and DIAMOND, with higher tiers being harder to catch.
|Slot|Name|Rarity|Requirement|Catch Chance|Pity GOLD|Pity DIAMOND|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Common Frog|COMMON ([[Rarity]])|Caught everywhere.|25% (1 in 4)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Exploding Frog|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Obtained by combining Lily Pads together until they get so big they EXPLODE!!|100% (1 in 1)|50|300|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Leap Frog|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Caught while midair.|20% (1 in 5)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Wetlands Frog|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Caught during rain.|15% (1 in 6.67)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Reality Hopper|UNCOMMON ([[Rarity]])|Caught in Wormholes ([[Wormhole]]) on the Lotus Atoll.|10% (1 in 10)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Blessed Frog|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Caught while having an active Blessing from throwing a [[Frogcoin]] into the waters of the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]].|10% (1 in 10)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Bullfrog|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Caught while wearing a [[Red Sweater]].|5% (1 in 20)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Sea Frog|RARE ([[Rarity]])|Caught while underwater.|5% (1 in 20)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Cave Frog|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Found in the [[Lotus Eater's Cave]].|2% (1 in 50)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Highlands Frog|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Found in the [[Lotus Highlands]].|2% (1 in 50)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Tree Frog|EPIC ([[Rarity]])|Caught while standing on leaves.|2% (1 in 50)|100|600|
|[[Trophy Frogs]]|Puddle Jumper|LEGENDARY ([[Rarity]])|Caught when flying around the Lotus Atoll. (Drops from the [[Puddle Jumper]] Sea Creature ([[Sea Creatures]]))|100% (1 in 1)|50|300|
### Wormholes
A Wormhole under a bridge near the spawn area.
[[Wormhole|Wormholes]] are purple particle rings that can be seen in the Lotus Atoll and [[Crimson Isle]] while wearing [[Froggles]], [[Silver Froggles]], [[Golden Froggles]], or [[Diamond Froggles]]. While fishing in them, players have a chance to catch the [[Trophy Frogs|Reality Hopper]] [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]]. Higher tier Froggles and the [[Geometric Oddity]] also grant [[Trophy Chance]] and higher chances at GOLD and DIAMOND [[Trophy Frogs]].
Additionally, fishing inside a [[Wormhole]] prevents [[Sea Creatures]] from being caught.
### Exploding Lily Pads
A large lily pad right before explosion.
The water in the Lotus Atoll not only contains [[Lily Pad|Lily Pads]] placed as blocks, but also lily pads that can be punched around on the water. If two Lily Pads of different sizes get close together, the smaller one will be absorbed by the larger one, which also increases the size of the larger Lily Pad. Once a single Lily Pad reaches size 8, it will float into the air, spin, and then explode. The explosion grants the following rewards to all players within 30 blocks who interacted with the Lily Pad (including merged Lily Pads) during the past 180 seconds:
- [[Trophy Frogs|Exploding Frog]] [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]]
- 120 (1 stack plus 56)–180 (2 stacks plus 52) [[Lily Pad|Lily Pads]][Confirm (Confirmation needed; When this info is confirmed, remove this.)]
- 60–100 (1 stack plus 36) [[Lotus]]
- +120 [[Fishing|Fishing XP]]
After a Lily Pad explodes, 3-5 [[Sea Creatures|Lotus Sea Creatures]] spawn based on the sum of the participating players' [[Tracking]] (up to 400 [Confirm]). Each Sea Creature targets a random participating player. There is also a 50% base chance to spawn a [[Flipflopper]] and a 25% base chance to spawn [[Seashine]], which can be increased by the participants' [[Tracking]].
[[Attributes|Lily Tracker]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] grants [[Tracking]] on Exploding Lily Pads and [[Attributes|Lily Puncher]] [[Attributes|Attribute]] gives more force when punching them.

An example of a hotspot in the Lotus Atoll.
### Hotspots
[[Fishing Hotspots|Hotspots]] appear in water to grant buffs and new [[Sea Creatures]] when the player's bobber is within.
### Weather
Every 40 minutes on the Lotus Atoll, 20 minutes of rain begins. While raining, players gain +25 [[Fishing Speed]] and can catch [[Trophy Frogs|Wetlands Frogs]].
Occasionally, rain may become a thunderstorm. It provides no additional effect.[Confirm]

# Lotus Eater's Cave
Categories: Locations
[Lotus Eater's Cave]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: Fishing X (10) ([[Fishing]])
Fairy Souls: 1
Location: [[Lotus Atoll]]
Coordinates: 90 66 -4
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[The Lotus Eater]]
[Resources]
Resources: Cave Frog ([[Trophy Frogs]])
The Lotus Eater's Cave is a [[Fishing]] [[Locations|Location]] on the [[Lotus Atoll]] and home of [[The Lotus Eater]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Lotus Eater's Cave, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Lotus Eater's Cave
■ Catch Cave Frogs.
■ Throw a Frogcoin into the pond for good luck!
## Purpose
### Fishing
The Lotus Eater's Cave serves as a place to go [[Fishing]] for [[Trophy Frogs|Cave Frogs]], a type of [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]].
### Frogcoin Donations
[[Frogcoin|Frogcoins]] can be thrown into the water of the Lotus Eater's Cave to donate it, granting 1 randomly chosen buffs, excluding ones already active, for 30 minutes while on the [[Lotus Atoll]]:
- Random Rewards
- +1 [[Treasure Chance]]
- +5 [[Trophy Chance]]
- +2.5 [[Sea Creature Chance]]
- +10 [[Fishing Speed]]
While under any of these effects, the player is able to catch the [[Trophy Frogs|Blessed Frog]] [[Trophy Frogs|Trophy Frog]]. Once a fourth [[Frogcoin]] is thrown, the [[The Lotus Eater]] NPC appears. Throwing multiple [[Frogcoin|Frogcoins]] at the same time only consumes one of them, while the others remain in the water. Attempting to throw a fifth [[Frogcoin]] does nothing.
#### Game Messages
|Condition|Message|
|-|-|
|Throwing the first to third Frogcoins ([[Frogcoin]])|WISE! You've been granted [stat] for 30m while on the Lotus Atoll!|
|Throwing the fourth coin|WISE! You've been granted [stat] for 30m while on the Lotus Atoll! The Lotus Eater beckons you from his cave...|
|If all the buffs are already active|The waters have denied your Frogcoin, offering nothing in return! But The Lotus Eater awaits in his cave...|
## NPCs

|[[The Lotus Eater]]|[[The Lotus Eater]]|Lotus Eater's Cave; 99.5, 68, -5.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Sells the [[Lily Pad Minion XII Upgrade Stone]].|✔|
## Fairy Souls

|No.|X|Y|Z|Description|Walkable?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|LA-1|86|65|-12|Inside the pool of water|✔|

## Tasks

Later on, [[Captain Ahone]], [[Grelius]], and [[Krevius]] appear, discussing stolen [[Sulphur]].

# Magma Fields
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows
[Magma Fields]
Type: [[Mining]] Location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Sub-locations: [[Khazad-dûm]]
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Crystal Hollows]]
Precise Location: At or below Y=64
[Inhabitants]
Mobs ([[Mob]]): [[Lava Blaze]] [[Lava Pigman]] [[Yog]]
[Resources]
Mob Drops: [[Blaze Rod]] [[Blazen Sphere]] [[Carmine Dye]] [[Eternal Flame Ring]] [[Magma Core]] Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Yoggie]]
Resources: [[Yoggie]] [[Hard Stone]] Rough Topaz Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]])
The Magma Fields are found in the [[Crystal Hollows]], where [[Yog|Yogs]] can spawn. Any location in the [[Crystal Hollows]] at or below [[Coordinates|X, 64, Z]] is considered part of the Magma Fields.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Magma Fields, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Magma Fields
■ Find Topaz Gemstones.
■ Watch your temperature!
## Purpose
- The player's [[Heat]] gradually rises until they begin to burn and take damage.
- [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] bypasses the [[Lava Talisman]] and [[Fire Talisman]]'s effects, but not the [[Campfire Badge]]'s effects.
- [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] allows for fishing [[Lava Blaze|Lava Blazes]] and [[Lava Pigman]], alongside its own Catches from its separate loot table.
- 4 of their entrances connect to the [[Crystal Nucleus]] and can house [[Khazad-dûm]].
## Game Messages
|Condition|Game Message|
|-|-|
|Entering the Magma Fields|DANGER; You have entered the Magma Fields|
|Reaching +90 [[Heat]]|The following titles sequentially appear, spaced by a few seconds: You are heating up! You are starting to burn! You are melting! Time to leave!|
|Dying from reaching +100 [[Heat]]|You burned to death.|
## Trivia
- Early on in the release of the [[Crystal Hollows]] (0.12), lava [[Fishing]] in the Magma Fields had vanilla Minecraft loot tables, leading to lots of glitched items.
- There is a type of random structure generated in Magma Fields which spawns [[Automaton|Automatons]] constantly regardless of the area it overlaps with.
- There is a secret [[Achievements|Achievement]] that can be obtained:
Show/Hide Achievement
Throwing an [[Eternal Flame Ring]] into the [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]] of a certain structure for the first time unlocks [[Achievements|The Ring]]

Matriarch's Lair is a location in the [[Magma Chamber]] below the [[Blazing Volcano]].

## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Matriarch's Lair, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Matriarch's Lair

## Description
[[The Matriarch]], a tentacle beast can be found here, which you can enter [[Belly of the Beast]], obtain and collect [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]].
## Trivia
- Up to 3 [[Heavy Pearl|Heavy Pearls]] through [[The Matriarch]] can be obtained every 24 hours. This limit can be bypassed by consuming the [[Matriarch's Perfume]]. This item can be consumed 2 times every 24 hours and can grant up to 9 Heavy Pearls.

# Melody's Plateau
Categories: Locations
[Melody's Plateau]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Location: [[Savanna Woodland]]
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Melody]]
Melody's Plateau is a sub-location of [[The Park]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Melody's Plateau, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Melody's Plateau
■ Talk to Melody ♫.
## Features
Melody's Plateau features a few attractions.
### Melody
[[Melody]] is an [[NPC]] located in Melody's Plateau, next to the acacia tree. The player can interact with her to select a song to play at the [[Harp]]. Perfecting a song allows the player to claim unique rewards from [[Melody]]. The player can also purchase a [[Personal Harp]] from [[Melody]] to play on their own [[Private Island]].
### Harp
The [[Harp]] is a feature located next to [[Melody]] in Melody's Plateau. The player can select one of many well-known, classical songs and play it. Doing so involves clicking on the falling wool blocks, to create the tune. Songs can be canceled at any time, but completing a song will display the percentage of correct clicks. A perfect score involves the player achieving 100%.

# Mines of Divan
Aliases: Mines Of Divan
Categories: Locations, Crystal Hollows, Mining
[Mines of Divan]
Type: [[Mining]] Location
Level: Mining XII (12) ([[Mining]]); [[Heart of the Mountain]] Tier 4
Music: "Going Even Deeper ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Mithril Deposits]]
Precise Location: Varies between lobbies
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): Keeper of Diamond ([[Keepers of Divan]]) Keeper of Emerald ([[Keepers of Divan]]) Keeper of Gold ([[Keepers of Divan]]) Keeper of Lapis ([[Keepers of Divan]])
The Mines of Divan is a location in the [[Crystal Hollows]], more specifically the [[Mithril Deposits]], where the [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]] can be obtained. Claiming the crystal requires a [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Emerald Hammer]], [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Diamond Axe]], [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Lapis Sword]], and a [[Scavenged Items|Scavenged Golden Hammer]]. Because these items are Quest items, they aren't able to be traded. Using a [[Wishing Compass]] in the [[Mithril Deposits]] before obtaining a [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]] points to the Mines of Divan.
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Mines of Divan, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Mines of Divan
■ Fear entering the mines.
■ The dwarves delved too greedily and too deep.
■ Treasures piled high, who knows what lies beneath.
## Purpose
Talking to any [[Keepers of Divan|Keeper]] gives the player a [[Metal Detector]]. While holding the Metal Detector, the player's distance from the treasure is shown, as well as increasingly louder/higher pitched beeps when nearing treasure. The treasure chest can contain certain items, including a Scavenged Item. The [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]] will appear in the center after delivering each Scavenged Item to their respective keepers. As you won't get Scavenged Items you already have in your inventory, you need to find 22.22 treasures on average to get one Crystal.
The treasure chest can reward either one of four [[Scavenged Items]] (18%), [[Mithril Powder]] (8%), [[Gemstone Powder]] (6%), (4x, 8x, 16x, 32x or 64x) Rough [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] (68%), and a rare chance of obtaining a [[Pickonimbus 2000]] (0.06%) or 2x Fine [[Gemstone|Gemstones]] (0.01%).
## NPCs
There are 4 [[NPC|NPCs]] found in the Mines of Divan:
|Image|NPC|Minimap|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Keepers of Divan]]|Keeper of Diamond ([[Keepers of Divan]])|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Accepts the Scavenged Diamond Axe ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).|✕|
|[[Keepers of Divan]]|Keeper of Emerald ([[Keepers of Divan]])|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Accepts the Scavenged Emerald Hammer ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).|✕|
|[[Keepers of Divan]]|Keeper of Gold ([[Keepers of Divan]])|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Accepts the Scavenged Golden Hammer ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).|✕|
|[[Keepers of Divan]]|Keeper of Lapis ([[Keepers of Divan]])|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Mines of Divan; Moves Around|Accepts the Scavenged Lapis Sword ([[Scavenged Items]]) from the player so they can obtain the Jade Crystal ([[Gemstone Crystals]]).|✕|
## Trivia
- It is also possible to mine the various valuable blocks (Pure Gold, Diamond, Emerald, Redstone) within the Mines for resources, and is a good way to get [[Mining|Mining XP]].
  - Coal blocks can also be found on the edges, however they do not give [[Mining|Mining XP]] and higher blocks are hard to reach, therefore it is generally not recommended to mine coal there.
  - Wearing [[Mineral Armor]] is the most effective way to clear out the gold blocks.
- Occasionally, the structure generation inside the Mines of Divan will stop certain [[Keepers of Divan|Keepers]] from spawning, and thus the crystal cannot be obtained.
  - This seems to mainly happen to the Keeper of Gold.
  - This bug has existed since at least September 2021.
- "The dwarves delved too greedily and too deep." is a reference to Saruman from The Lord of the Rings describing a creature the dwarves awoke in the depths of Khazad-dûm.
- This structure contains a massive amount of Jade Gemstone ores, mostly full blocks rather than panes, which makes it an ideal place to collect Jades and make coins.
## Gallery
- The Jade Crystal The [[Gemstone Crystals|Jade Crystal]]

# Mirrorverse
Aliases: Button Puzzle, Button puzzle, Dance Room, Dance room, Mirrorcosm
Categories: Locations, Pages with UIs
[Mirrorverse]
Type: Location ([[Locations]])
Level: SkyBlock Level 12 ([[SkyBlock Levels]])
Music: "Rift ([[Hypixel SkyBlock OST]])"
Location: [[Rift Dimension]]
← Previous Location: [[West Village]]
Next Location →: None
[Inhabitants]
NPCs ([[NPC]]): [[Dr. Emmett]]
[Metadata]
ID: MIRRORVERSE
The Mirrorverse is a location in the [[Rift Dimension]].
## Features
The Mirrorverse features many attractions. The player must have secured the [[Supreme Timecharm]] and have at least 3:00 [[Rift Time]] remaining in order to access the location. While inside the Mirrorverse, the player's [[Rift Time]] is frozen. A full guide can be found here.
### First Puzzle
The first room requires the player to make all the levers match their state in the mirror. The player's side of the floor is blank. In the mirror's reflection are four levers, flipped in different directions. The player must flip all the levers on their side to face upwards, opening the [[Iron Door]] that leads to the second puzzle.
By talking with [[Dr. Emmett]] in this room, you can fast travel to any Puzzle that has been previously completed, including the reward room.
### Second Puzzle
The second room requires the player to follow a path to cross the floor. The player's side of the floor is blank. In the mirror's reflection is [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|lava]], with a winding path of [[Block of Iron|Iron Blocks]]. The player must follow the path without stepping into lava. If the player steps onto lava, it will send the player back to the beginning.
### Third Puzzle
The third room requires the player to craft a [[RemmaH yniT]]. The player's side of the room is blank. In the mirror's reflection there is a [[Crafting Table]] located in front of the mirror and multiple mobs scattered across the floor. The player must kill the mobs based on their mirrored location in order to obtain the drops needed to craft multiple items.
Baby zombies will drop [[KnalP dooW kaO]], cave spiders drop [[GnirtS]] and slimes will drop [[LlabemilS]]. All the drops can be crafted into items, which can then be crafted into a [[RemmaH yniT]].
### Fourth Puzzle
The fourth room requires the player to cross a room using parkour. The player's side of the room is filled with wooden platforms, with [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|lava]] underneath. In the mirror's reflection, which now lays above the main area, there is only a selection of platforms, consisting of those that are safe for the player to stand on to cross the room. Approaching an incorrect platform will cause it to vanish. If the player comes in contact with [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|lava]], they will be flung into the air. If the player keeps falling in, they get sent back to the beginning with the following message:
OH NO! THE LAVA OOFED YOU BACK TO THE START!
#### Hard Mode
At the beginning of the puzzle there is a wooden button on the wall, which when toggled, will turn on hard mode. During hard mode, the player will not be launched up in the air if they touch [[Vanilla Objects/Blocks|Lava]], and standing on a correct platform for too long will cause it to collapse. As a reward for completing the entire puzzle on hard mode, however, the player will be allowed to open the chest at the end, which contains a [[Test Bucket Please Ignore]].
### Fifth Puzzle
The fifth room consists of four rooms which requires the player to open an [[Iron Door]] at the end of each room. On either side of the room is a mirror. The player can click on either to switch to that one, and freeze the other. The last room is completely optional, and when entered will trigger the following message:
AYAYAY! Does your brain hurt?
Fortunately, this room is 100% optional!
Click the button at the end of the room to skip it!
Deciding to complete the last room will unlock the chest, which contains a [[Big Brain Talisman]].
### Sixth Puzzle
The sixth room, one of the most ping-dependent rooms, requires the player to move from block to block that appears in a square formation on the ground. A set order will appear on-screen, and the player must obey it or else the puzzle starts again. Orders that appear involve the following, as well as combinations of them:
- Move!
- Sneak!
- Stand!
- Jump!
- Don't jump!
- Punch!
Sequence:
|Cycle|Blue|Red|Pink|Green|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|1|Move!|Move!|Move!|Move!|
|2|Sneak!|Stand!|Sneak!|Stand!|
|3|Sneak!|Stand!|Sneak!|Stand!|
|4|Sneak! and Jump!|Stand! and Jump!|Sneak! and Don't jump!|Stand! and Don't jump!|
|5|Sneak! and Jump!|Stand! and Jump!|Sneak! and Don't jump!|Stand! and Don't jump!|
|6|Sneak! and Jump!|Stand! and Jump!|Sneak! and Don't jump!|Stand! and Don't jump!|
|7|Sneak! and Jump!|Stand! and Jump!|Sneak! and Don't jump!|Stand! and Don't jump!|
|8|Sneak! and Jump!|Stand! and Jump!|Sneak! and Don't jump!|Stand! and Don't jump!|
|9|Sneak! and Jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Jump! and Punch!|Sneak! and Don't jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Don't jump! and Punch!|
|10|Sneak! and Jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Jump! and Punch!|Sneak! and Don't jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Don't jump! and Punch!|
|11|Sneak! and Jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Jump! and Punch!|Sneak! and Don't jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Don't jump! and Punch!|
|12|Sneak! and Jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Jump! and Punch!|Sneak! and Don't jump! and Punch!|Stand! and Don't jump! and Punch!|
After progressing through the dance past row 7 of the table above, a hole will appear in the center of the room, leading to the seventh puzzle, and a message will appear in chat, saying:
You completed the dance!
Keep going to earn the Tiny Dancer talisman!
#### Full Dance
The player can decide to stop dancing, jump into the hole that has appeared and continue with Mirrorverse or continue dancing to receive the Tiny Dancer. Continuing for enough time will grant the player the [[Tiny Dancer]] [[Accessories|accessory]], and the following message will appear:
You completed the full dance! Amazing!
### Seventh Puzzle
The seventh room requires the player to complete a vertical parkour course. The player's side of the room is blank. In the mirror's reflection there is a selection of [[Planks|Oak Planks]], consisting of those that are safe for the player to stand on.
At the beginning of the parkour the player is advised to claim an [[Emmett Pointer]] from [[Dr. Emmett]]. This can be used to greatly aid the player in completing the parkour. Aiming the Pointer at a block in the mirror will shoot a green laser through the mirror into the player's side, highlighting the invisible blocks.
There are 3 mirrors, each on a different wall, and reaching each one indicates a checkpoint. Once the player reaches a checkpoint, they can teleport to it whenever they want by using the levers on the ground. Completing the full puzzle without using checkpoints will allow the player to open the chest at the end of the parkour, which contains a [[Miniaturized Tubulator]].
### Eighth Room
There is no puzzle in the eighth room, instead just [[Dr. Emmett]], who will congratulate the player on completing the Mirrorverse. The player can claim 60,000 [[Motes]] once and can purchase the [[MrahcemiT esrevrorriM]], [[EtalptsehC norI]] and [[Enchanted Book Bundle|Reflection I]] [[Enchanted Book Bundle|Enchanted Book Bundles]] at a cost.
## NPCs

|Image|NPC|Location|Description|Shop?|
|-|-|-|-|-|
|[[Dr. Emmett]]|[[Dr. Emmett]]|Mirrorverse; -81, 64, -114 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here in the first room.|✔|
|[[Dr. Emmett]]|[[Dr. Emmett]]|Mirrorverse; -82.5, 51, -115.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here in the second room.|✔|
|[[Dr. Emmett]]|[[Dr. Emmett]]|Mirrorverse; -117, 39, -108 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here in the fourth room.|✔|
|[[Dr. Emmett]]|[[Dr. Emmett]]|Mirrorverse; -225.5, 43, -104.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here in the fifth room.|✔|
|[[Dr. Emmett]]|[[Dr. Emmett]]|Mirrorverse; -241.5, 43, -108.5 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here in the fifth room.|✔|
|[[Dr. Emmett]]|[[Dr. Emmett]]|Mirrorverse; -260, 43, -105 ([[Coordinates]])|Appears here in the fifth room.|✔|

Mob Drops: [[Corleonite]] [[Premium Flesh]] [[Revive Stone]] Rough Jade Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) Rough Ruby Gemstone ([[Gemstone]]) [[Treasurite]] [[Undead Essence]]
Resources: [[Hard Stone]] [[Gemstone]]
The Mithril Deposits are a randomly-generated location in the [[Crystal Hollows]]. Farmland is used as the ground, and minecart tracks are often found running along the pathways. The [[Mines of Divan]] spawns in a random location inside, as well as some [[Crystal Hollows/Special Locations|other structures]].
## Tasks
When the [[Player]] first enters Mithril Deposits, they will be greeted with the following message:
NEW AREA DISCOVERED!
Mithril Deposits
■ A familiar-looking place rich with Jade Gemstones.
